<script id="ethvelle-v69-zoom-audio-final-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number(v)||0));
  const selectedAudio=()=>{try{return Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>o&&o.type==='audio'&&String(o.id)===String(selectedClip))||null:null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  const isAnimationSelected=()=>selectedClip==='main'||(typeof selectedClip==='string'&&selectedClip.startsWith('seg'));

  /* Replace the bottom Custom tool with a zoom control alongside the transport keyframe button. */
  window.toggleCanvasZoomView=function(){
    const active=!!window.__ethvelleCustomAnimationMode;
    if(active){if(typeof window.closeCustomAnimationStudio==='function')window.closeCustomAnimationStudio();}
    else if(typeof window.openCustomAnimationStudio==='function')window.openCustomAnimationStudio();
    const btn=$('transportZoomBtn');if(btn){btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(!active));btn.title=active?'Zoom canvas':'Exit canvas zoom';}
  };
  const oldOpenStudio=window.openCustomAnimationStudio;
  if(typeof oldOpenStudio==='function'&&!oldOpenStudio.__evcaV69){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldOpenStudio.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      const hud=$('evcaCustomMotionHud');if(hud){const title=hud.querySelector('.evca-motion-title');if(title)title.textContent='Canvas Zoom';const exit=hud.querySelector('.evca-motion-top .evca-motion-btn');if(exit)exit.textContent='Normal Model';}
      const btn=$('transportZoomBtn');if(btn){btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');btn.title='Exit canvas zoom';}
    });return result;};
    wrapped.__evcaV69=true;window.openCustomAnimationStudio=wrapped;
  }
  const oldCloseStudio=window.closeCustomAnimationStudio;
  if(typeof oldCloseStudio==='function'&&!oldCloseStudio.__evcaV69){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldCloseStudio.apply(this,arguments);const btn=$('transportZoomBtn');if(btn){btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');btn.title='Zoom canvas';}return result;};
    wrapped.__evcaV69=true;window.closeCustomAnimationStudio=wrapped;
  }
  function syncZoomButton(){
    const legacyCustomTool=$('clipCustomAnimTool');if(legacyCustomTool)legacyCustomTool.remove();
    const btn=$('transportZoomBtn');if(!btn)return;
    const show=isAnimationSelected();
    btn.style.display='inline-flex';
    btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(!!window.__ethvelleCustomAnimationMode));
  }
  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__evcaV69){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);syncZoomButton();return result;};
    wrapped.__evcaV69=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  const oldSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect==='function'&&!oldSelect.__evcaV69){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldSelect.apply(this,arguments);syncZoomButton();return result;};
    wrapped.__evcaV69=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }
  /* Ensure transport button sync after entering/leaving the editor and after DOM construction. */
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',syncZoomButton,{once:true});else syncZoomButton();

  /* Final audio panel renderer: toggles are preferences only; expensive PCM processing starts
     exclusively when the user presses the dedicated Apply button. */
  let activeAudioPanel='';
  const audioDefs={
    audioEffects:{title:'Audio Effects',property:'effect',items:[['none','Original'],['echo','Echo'],['studio','Studio'],['child','Child'],['boy','Boy'],['girl','Girl'],['woman','Woman'],['man','Man'],['oldman','Old Man'],['giant','Giant'],['monster','Monster'],['demon','Demon'],['ghost','Ghost'],['alien','Alien'],['chipmunk','Chipmunk'],['fairy','Fairy'],['cartoon','Cartoon'],['goblin','Goblin'],['dragon','Dragon'],['villain','Villain'],['zombie','Zombie'],['reverb','Reverb'],['hall','Hall'],['cathedral','Cathedral'],['slapback','Slapback'],['pingpong','Ping-Pong'],['stadium','Stadium'],['chorus','Chorus'],['flanger','Flanger'],['tremolo','Tremolo'],['robot','Robot'],['underwater','Underwater'],['megaphone','Megaphone'],['overdrive','Overdrive'],['lofi','Lo-Fi']]},
    audioVoice:{title:'Voice Filters',property:'voice',items:[['natural','Natural'],['clear','Clear'],['warm','Warm'],['deep','Deep'],['bright','Bright'],['radio','Radio'],['telephone','Telephone'],['soft','Soft'],['podcast','Podcast'],['broadcast','Broadcast'],['narrator','Narrator'],['rich','Rich'],['crisp','Crisp'],['cinematic','Cinematic'],['air','Air'],['smooth','Smooth'],['nasal','Nasal'],['hollow','Hollow'],['muffled','Muffled'],['walkie','Walkie-Talkie'],['intercom','Intercom'],['vintage','Vintage']]}
  };
  function getCfg(clip){return window.getEthvelleAudioSettings?window.getEthvelleAudioSettings(clip):(clip.audioSettings=Object.assign({effect:'none',voice:'natural',pitch:0,pan:0,fadeIn:0,fadeOut:0,enhance:55,enhanceEnabled:false,noiseReduction:false,noiseStrength:65},clip.audioSettings||{}));}
  function choiceGrid(key,cfg){const d=audioDefs[key];return `<div class="evca-audio-panel"><div class="evca-audio-panel-title">${d.title}</div><div class="evca-audio-choice-grid">${d.items.map(([v,label])=>`<button type="button" class="evca-audio-choice ${cfg[d.property]===v?'active':''}" onclick="setEthvelleAudioSetting('${d.property}','${v}',true)">${label}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>`;}
  function slider(id,label,value,min,max,step,unit,property){
    const formatted=unit==='st'?(Number(value)>0?'+':'')+Number(value).toFixed(1)+' st':unit==='s'?Number(value).toFixed(1)+' s':unit==='pan'?(Number(value)<0?'L '+Math.abs(Math.round(value))+'%':Number(value)>0?'R '+Math.round(value)+'%':'Center'):Math.round(value)+'%';
    return `<div class="evca-audio-slider-card"><div class="evca-audio-slider-head"><label for="${id}">${label}</label><output id="${id}Value">${formatted}</output></div><input class="evca-audio-slider" id="${id}" type="range" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step}" value="${value}" oninput="setEthvelleAudioSetting('${property}',this.value,false);document.getElementById('${id}Value').textContent=${unit==='st'?'(Number(this.value)>0?\'+\':\'\')+Number(this.value).toFixed(1)+\' st\'':unit==='s'?'Number(this.value).toFixed(1)+\' s\'':unit==='pan'?'(Number(this.value)<0?\'L \'+Math.abs(Math.round(Number(this.value)))+\'%\':Number(this.value)>0?\'R \'+Math.round(Number(this.value))+\'%\':\'Center\')':"Math.round(Number(this.value))+'%'"}" onchange="setEthvelleAudioSetting('${property}',this.value,true)"></div>`;
  }
  function switchField(label,property,checked){return `<label class="evca-small-switch"><span>${label}</span><input type="checkbox" ${checked?'checked':''} onchange="setEthvelleAudioSetting('${property}',this.checked,true)"><i></i></label>`;}
  function wirePanelRanges(host){
    if(!host)return;
    host.querySelectorAll('input[type="range"]').forEach(input=>{
      const paint=()=>{const min=Number(input.min)||0,max=Number(input.max)||100,val=Number(input.value)||0;input.style.setProperty('--evca-progress',clamp(max>min?(val-min)/(max-min)*100:0,0,100)+'%');};
      if(!input.dataset.evcaV69Range){input.addEventListener('input',paint);input.dataset.evcaV69Range='1';}
      paint();
    });
  }
  window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel=function(key){
    const host=$('evcaAudioPanel'),clip=selectedAudio();if(!host)return;
    if(!clip){host.innerHTML='';return;}
    activeAudioPanel=key||activeAudioPanel;const cfg=getCfg(clip);
    if(audioDefs[key]){host.innerHTML=choiceGrid(key,cfg);return;}
    if(key==='audioAdjust'){
      host.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-audio-panel"><div class="evca-audio-panel-title">Audio Adjustment</div>${slider('evcaAudioPitchV69','Pitch',cfg.pitch,-12,12,.5,'st','pitch')}${slider('evcaAudioPanV69','Stereo Balance',cfg.pan,-100,100,1,'pan','pan')}${slider('evcaAudioFadeInV69','Fade In',cfg.fadeIn,0,8,.1,'s','fadeIn')}${slider('evcaAudioFadeOutV69','Fade Out',cfg.fadeOut,0,8,.1,'s','fadeOut')}</div>`;wirePanelRanges(host);return;
    }
    if(key==='audioEnhance'){
      host.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-audio-panel">${switchField('Enable enhancement','enhanceEnabled',!!cfg.enhanceEnabled)}${slider('evcaAudioEnhanceV69','Enhance Amount',cfg.enhance,0,100,1,'%','enhance')}<button type="button" class="evca-audio-apply-btn" ${cfg.enhanceEnabled?'':'disabled'} onclick="ethvelleApplyAudioProcess('enhance')">Apply Enhancement</button></div>`;wirePanelRanges(host);return;
    }
    if(key==='audioNoise'){
      host.innerHTML=`<div class="evca-audio-panel">${switchField('Remove background noise','noiseReduction',!!cfg.noiseReduction)}${slider('evcaAudioNoiseV69','Reduction Strength',cfg.noiseStrength,0,100,1,'%','noiseStrength')}<button type="button" class="evca-audio-apply-btn" ${cfg.noiseReduction?'':'disabled'} onclick="ethvelleApplyAudioProcess('noise')">Apply Noise Reduction</button></div>`;wirePanelRanges(host);return;
    }
  };
  function refreshLiveAudio(clip){
    try{
      const now=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
      const t=now>=Number(clip.trimStart)&&now<Number(clip.trimEnd)?now:Math.max(0,Number(clip.trimStart)||0)+.01;
      if(typeof window.renderAudioOverlayMedia==='function')window.renderAudioOverlayMedia(t);
    }catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle audio preview update:',e);}
  }
  function restartAudioOnce(clip){
    try{
      if(typeof window.pausePlayback==='function')window.pausePlayback();
      if(typeof window.setCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function')window.setCurrentAbsoluteTime(Math.max(0,Number(clip.trimStart)||0));
      if(typeof window.renderFrame==='function')window.renderFrame();
      if(typeof window.startPlayback==='function')window.startPlayback();
      // Re-arm selected clip UI after the player has started; this is one single restart call.
      selectedClip=clip.id;selectedKfIdx=null;
      requestAnimationFrame(()=>{document.querySelectorAll('.overlay-clip.selected').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('selected'));const node=[...document.querySelectorAll('.overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]')].find(el=>String(el.getAttribute('data-overlay-id'))===String(clip.id));if(node)node.classList.add('selected');if(typeof window.syncBottomToolsMode==='function')window.syncBottomToolsMode();});
    }catch(e){console.warn('Audio autoplay:',e);}
  }
  window.setEthvelleAudioSetting=function(property,value,commit){
    const clip=selectedAudio();if(!clip)return;
    const cfg=getCfg(clip);
    if(property==='effect'&&(window.__EV_AUDIO_EFFECT_KEYS||['none','echo','studio']).includes(String(value)))cfg.effect=String(value);
    else if(property==='voice')cfg.voice=String(value);
    else if(property==='pitch')cfg.pitch=clamp(value,-12,12);
    else if(property==='pan')cfg.pan=clamp(value,-100,100);
    else if(property==='fadeIn'||property==='fadeOut')cfg[property]=clamp(value,0,8);
    else if(property==='enhance')cfg.enhance=clamp(value,0,100);
    else if(property==='enhanceEnabled')cfg.enhanceEnabled=!!value;
    else if(property==='noiseStrength')cfg.noiseStrength=clamp(value,0,100);
    else if(property==='noiseReduction')cfg.noiseReduction=!!value;
    refreshLiveAudio(clip);
    if(commit){try{if(typeof window.persistProjectState==='function')window.persistProjectState();}catch(e){console.warn('Audio setting save:',e);}if(activeAudioPanel)window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel(activeAudioPanel);
      // Selecting an effect/voice preset restarts playback exactly once from this clip's start.
      if(property==='effect'||property==='voice')restartAudioOnce(clip);
    }
  };
  const processBase=window.ethvelleProcessSelectedAudio;
  window.ethvelleApplyAudioProcess=async function(mode){
    const clip=selectedAudio();if(!clip||typeof processBase!=='function')return;
    const cfg=getCfg(clip);
    if(mode==='enhance'&&!cfg.enhanceEnabled){if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Enable enhancement before applying',{duration:1500});return;}
    if(mode==='noise'&&!cfg.noiseReduction){if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Enable noise reduction before applying',{duration:1500});return;}
    await window.ethvelleProcessSelectedAudio(mode);
  };
  if(typeof processBase==='function'&&!processBase.__evcaV69){
    const wrapped=async function(mode){const clip=selectedAudio();const beforeMode=clip&&clip.audioProcessedMode;const beforeSrc=clip&&clip.src;const beforeAsset=clip&&clip.audioProcessedAssetKey;await processBase.apply(this,arguments);const applied=!!(clip&&clip.audioProcessedMode===mode&&(beforeMode!==mode||clip.src!==beforeSrc||clip.audioProcessedAssetKey!==beforeAsset));if(applied&&typeof window.closePanel==='function')window.closePanel();};
    wrapped.__evcaV69=true;window.ethvelleProcessSelectedAudio=wrapped;
    window.ethvelleApplyAudioProcess=async function(mode){const clip=selectedAudio();if(!clip)return;const cfg=getCfg(clip);if(mode==='enhance'&&!cfg.enhanceEnabled){if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Enable enhancement before applying',{duration:1500});return;}if(mode==='noise'&&!cfg.noiseReduction){if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Enable noise reduction before applying',{duration:1500});return;}return window.ethvelleProcessSelectedAudio(mode);};
  }
  /* Give all audio panel openings the v69 renderer after the legacy panel shell is created. */
  const openBase=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof openBase==='function'&&!openBase.__evcaV69){
    const wrapped=function(key){const result=openBase.apply(this,arguments);if(['audioEffects','audioVoice','audioAdjust','audioEnhance','audioNoise'].includes(key)){requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.renderEthvelleAudioPanel(key));}return result;};
    wrapped.__evcaV69=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;
  }
  document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
    if(e.target.closest('#evcaCustomMotionHud .evca-motion-top .evca-motion-btn')){
      setTimeout(()=>{const btn=$('transportZoomBtn');if(btn){btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');btn.title='Zoom canvas';}},0);
    }
  },true);
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v411-scene-effects-pack">
/* Ethvelle v411 - extra VIDEO effects: 3 fire styles, 2 water-wave styles, 5 coloured smokes and
   5 fighting effects (round 2D cartoon smoke, impact bursts, speed lines, shock rings).
   Every effect is drawn procedurally from time only, so the live canvas, the timeline preview and the
   exported video all match. drawSceneFx() hands the pack: (ctx, type, tm, w, h, opts). */
(function(){
  'use strict';
  if(window.__evSceneFxPack)return;
  const TAU=Math.PI*2;
  const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v));
  const fr=x=>x-Math.floor(x);
  const lerp=(a,b,t)=>a+(b-a)*t;
  const ease=t=>{t=clamp(t,0,1);return t*t*(3-2*t);};
  const rgba=(c,a)=>'rgba('+c[0]+','+c[1]+','+c[2]+','+clamp(a,0,1)+')';
  const hs=i=>fr(Math.sin((i+1)*127.1+37.7)*43758.5453);

  /* ---------- catalogue: id, label, group ---------- */
  const TYPES=[]; /* v435: inbuilt scene effects removed - effects now come only from the admin Effects library */
  const LABEL={},GLYPH={};
  TYPES.forEach(t=>{LABEL[t[0]]=t[1];GLYPH[t[0]]=t[2]==='fire'?'🔥':t[2]==='water'?'🌊':t[2]==='smoke'?'☁':'💥';});

  /* ---------- FIRE ---------- */
  function flamePath(ctx,x,by,fw,fh,tm,seed){
    const sway=Math.sin(tm*6+seed*5.3)*fw*.55+Math.sin(tm*11+seed*2.1)*fw*.25;
    const hh=fh*(.82+.18*Math.sin(tm*8+seed*7.7));
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-fw,by);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x-fw*1.25,by-hh*.34,x-fw*.3+sway*.5,by-hh*.64,x+sway,by-hh);
    ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+fw*.3+sway*.5,by-hh*.6,x+fw*1.25,by-hh*.34,x+fw,by);
    ctx.closePath();
  }
  const FIRE_PAL={
    fireblaze:{glow:[255,110,20],outer:[[220,40,10],[255,110,20]],mid:[[255,120,25],[255,190,60]],core:[[255,210,80],[255,250,200]],ember:[255,200,90],hFrac:.46,count:11},
    fireinferno:{glow:[255,60,10],outer:[[120,10,6],[225,40,10]],mid:[[225,55,12],[255,135,25]],core:[[255,150,30],[255,232,130]],ember:[255,140,40],hFrac:.74,count:9,smoke:true},
    fireblue:{glow:[60,140,255],outer:[[20,50,200],[50,120,255]],mid:[[50,130,255],[110,200,255]],core:[[130,215,255],[235,250,255]],ember:[150,215,255],hFrac:.5,count:11}
  };
  function drawFire(ctx,type,tm,w,h,o){
    const P=FIRE_PAL[type],s=o.strength,n=clamp(Math.round(P.count*o.density/1.35),5,22);
    const fh=h*P.hFrac*clamp(.8+o.scale*.2,.6,1.6),by=h*1.02,sw=w/n;
    ctx.save();
    // heat glow rising from the bottom edge
    const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,h,0,h-fh*1.25);
    g.addColorStop(0,rgba(P.glow,clamp(.5*s/1.9,.18,.8)));g.addColorStop(.55,rgba(P.glow,.16*s/1.9));g.addColorStop(1,rgba(P.glow,0));
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(0,h-fh*1.3,w,fh*1.3+2);
    if(P.smoke){ // dark smoke ceiling above the inferno
      const sg=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,h-fh*1.2,0,h-fh*.5);sg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(10,8,8,0)');sg.addColorStop(.5,'rgba(18,14,14,'+clamp(.3*s/1.9,.1,.5)+')');sg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(30,14,10,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle=sg;ctx.fillRect(0,h-fh*1.2,w,fh*.8);
    }
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';
    const passes=[[P.outer,1,1],[P.mid,.72,.78],[P.core,.42,.5]];
    passes.forEach((ps,pi)=>{
      const col=ps[0],wm=ps[1],hm=ps[2];
      const gr=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,by,0,by-fh*hm);
      gr.addColorStop(0,rgba(col[0],clamp(.92*s/1.9,.35,1)));gr.addColorStop(1,rgba(col[1],clamp(.55*s/1.9,.2,.9)));
      ctx.fillStyle=gr;
      for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
        const x=(i+.5)*sw+(hs(i+pi*11)-.5)*sw*.5;
        const hv=.62+.55*hs(i*3+5)+(i%3===0?.18:0);
        flamePath(ctx,x,by,sw*.78*wm,fh*hv*hm,tm,i+pi*3.7);ctx.fill();
      }
    });
    // second, taller tongues that dance between the main row
    const tongues=Math.round(n*.45);
    for(let i=0;i<tongues;i++){
      const x=w*(hs(i+40)*.9+.05)+Math.sin(tm*1.4+i)*sw*.35;
      const gr=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,by,0,by-fh*1.15);gr.addColorStop(0,rgba(P.mid[0],.7*clamp(s/1.9,.4,1)));gr.addColorStop(1,rgba(P.mid[1],0));
      ctx.fillStyle=gr;flamePath(ctx,x,by,sw*.5,fh*(.85+.35*hs(i+60)),tm*1.15,i+90);ctx.fill();
    }
    // rising embers / sparks
    const em=Math.round(26*o.density);
    for(let i=0;i<em;i++){
      const sp=.16+.2*hs(i+7),p=fr(tm*sp+hs(i+13)),x=hs(i+21)*w+Math.sin(tm*2.3+i)*w*.025,y=h*1.0-p*fh*1.75;
      const r=Math.max(.8,w*.0042*(1-p*.65)*(.6+hs(i+3)));
      ctx.fillStyle=rgba(P.ember,Math.sin(p*Math.PI)*.95*clamp(s/1.9,.4,1));
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  /* ---------- WATER WAVES ---------- */
  function waveLayer(ctx,w,h,tm,y0,amp,len,spd,top,bot,foam,phase){
    const steps=Math.max(24,Math.round(w/14));
    const yAt=x=>y0+Math.sin(x/len*TAU+tm*spd+phase)*amp+Math.sin(x/(len*.55)*TAU-tm*spd*.8+phase*1.7)*amp*.45;
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,h+4);
    for(let i=0;i<=steps;i++){const x=i/steps*w;ctx.lineTo(x,yAt(x));}
    ctx.lineTo(w,h+4);ctx.closePath();
    const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,y0-amp*1.4,0,h);g.addColorStop(0,top);g.addColorStop(1,bot);
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fill();
    if(foam>0){
      ctx.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<=steps;i++){const x=i/steps*w,y=yAt(x);i?ctx.lineTo(x,y):ctx.moveTo(x,y);}
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(235,248,255,'+foam+')';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.2,h*.006);ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.stroke();
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,'+(foam*.55)+')';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(.8,h*.0025);
      ctx.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<=steps;i++){const x=i/steps*w,y=yAt(x)+h*.012;i?ctx.lineTo(x,y):ctx.moveTo(x,y);}ctx.stroke();
    }
    return yAt;
  }
  function drawWaves(ctx,type,tm,w,h,o){
    const big=type==='wavesurge',s=clamp(o.strength/1.9,.35,1.4),sc=clamp(.75+o.scale*.25,.6,1.5);
    const base=big?h*.5:h*.7,amp=h*(big?.055:.03)*sc,len=w*(big?.62:.5);
    const layers=big?5:4;
    ctx.save();
    // soft blue water tint so the surface reads on any footage
    const tint=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,base-amp*3,0,h);tint.addColorStop(0,'rgba(40,130,230,0)');tint.addColorStop(1,'rgba(10,70,170,'+clamp(.3*s,.1,.45)+')');
    ctx.fillStyle=tint;ctx.fillRect(0,base-amp*3,w,h-base+amp*3);
    const crest=[];
    for(let L=0;L<layers;L++){
      const f=L/(layers-1),y0=base+f*(h-base)*.55-amp*.5;
      const top='rgba('+Math.round(lerp(120,30,f))+','+Math.round(lerp(205,120,f))+','+Math.round(lerp(255,215,f))+','+clamp((.34+.1*f)*s,.15,.7)+')';
      const bot='rgba('+Math.round(lerp(30,8,f))+','+Math.round(lerp(110,60,f))+','+Math.round(lerp(210,150,f))+','+clamp((.5+.1*f)*s,.2,.8)+')';
      const fn=waveLayer(ctx,w,h,tm*(1+f*.25),y0,amp*(1-f*.28),len*(1-f*.18),(1.15+f*.55)*(L%2?-1:1)*1,top,bot,clamp((.85-f*.5)*s,.2,.95),L*1.9);
      crest.push([fn,y0,amp]);
    }
    // sun glints running along the crests + rising bubbles
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';
    const gl=Math.round(26*o.density);
    for(let i=0;i<gl;i++){
      const lay=crest[i%crest.length],x=fr(hs(i+3)+tm*.02*(1+hs(i)))*w,y=lay[0](x)+h*.01;
      const tw=.5+.5*Math.sin(tm*4.2+i*2.3);
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(220,244,255,'+(.5*tw*s)+')';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,y,w*.012*(.6+tw),h*.0035,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    }
    for(let i=0;i<Math.round(14*o.density);i++){
      const p=fr(tm*(.07+.06*hs(i))+hs(i+9)),x=hs(i+17)*w+Math.sin(tm*1.6+i)*w*.02,y=h-p*(h-base)*1.2,r=Math.max(1,w*.006*(.5+hs(i+4)));
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(210,240,255,'+(Math.sin(p*Math.PI)*.8*s)+')';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(.7,r*.35);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,TAU);ctx.stroke();
    }
    if(big){ // white-caps that burst off the front crest
      const lay=crest[0];
      for(let i=0;i<10;i++){
        const p=fr(tm*.25+i/10),x=(i/10+.04*Math.sin(tm+i))*w,y=lay[0](x)-p*h*.07;
        ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,'+(Math.sin(p*Math.PI)*.55*s)+')';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,w*.01*(1-p*.5),0,TAU);ctx.fill();
      }
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  /* ---------- COLOURED SMOKE ---------- */
  const SMOKE_PAL={
    smokegray:{a:[96,100,110],b:[44,47,55],edge:[150,154,162],base:[60,63,70],alpha:.62,count:15},
    smokewhite:{a:[255,255,255],b:[214,222,232],edge:[255,255,255],base:[235,240,247],alpha:.58,count:15},
    smokeblack:{a:[34,34,40],b:[6,6,9],edge:[70,70,80],base:[10,10,14],alpha:.72,count:15},
    smokered:{a:[235,70,60],b:[130,16,20],edge:[255,150,130],base:[150,20,24],alpha:.6,count:14},
    smokepurple:{a:[176,100,240],b:[70,24,130],edge:[225,180,255],base:[85,30,150],alpha:.6,count:14}
  };
  function drawSmoke(ctx,type,tm,w,h,o){
    const P=SMOKE_PAL[type],s=clamp(o.strength/1.9,.35,1.5),n=clamp(Math.round(P.count*o.density/1.35),7,26),sc=clamp(.8+o.scale*.2,.6,1.6);
    ctx.save();
    // low ground haze
    const hz=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,h,0,h*.45);hz.addColorStop(0,rgba(P.base,clamp(.42*s,.12,.7)));hz.addColorStop(1,rgba(P.base,0));
    ctx.fillStyle=hz;ctx.fillRect(0,h*.4,w,h*.62);
    const parts=[];
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
      const life=.085+.06*hs(i+2),p=fr(tm*life+hs(i+9)),seedX=hs(i+5);
      parts.push({p,i,x:seedX*w+Math.sin(tm*.55+i*1.7)*w*.06+p*w*(hs(i+31)-.5)*.3,y:h*(1.08-p*1.18),r:h*(.09+.2*p)*sc*(.75+.5*hs(i+14)),a:Math.sin(Math.pow(p,.8)*Math.PI)});
    }
    parts.sort((u,v)=>u.p-v.p);
    parts.forEach(q=>{
      const al=clamp(P.alpha*s*q.a,0,.9);if(al<=.01)return;
      const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(q.x-q.r*.2,q.y-q.r*.25,q.r*.05,q.x,q.y,q.r);
      g.addColorStop(0,rgba(P.edge,al*.55));g.addColorStop(.28,rgba(P.a,al));g.addColorStop(.72,rgba(P.b,al*.8));g.addColorStop(1,rgba(P.b,0));
      ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(q.x,q.y,q.r,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
      // swirl lobes make each puff billow instead of looking like a flat disc
      const lob=rgba(P.a,al*.5);ctx.fillStyle=lob;
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(q.x+Math.cos(tm*.8+q.i)*q.r*.42,q.y-q.r*.28+Math.sin(tm*.7+q.i)*q.r*.12,q.r*.5,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    });
    ctx.restore();
  }

  /* ---------- FIGHTING (2D cartoon) ---------- */
  function cartoonPuff(ctx,x,y,r,fill,line,lw){
    ctx.beginPath();
    for(let k=0;k<5;k++){const a=k*TAU/5+.3;ctx.moveTo(x+Math.cos(a)*r*.55+r*.5,y+Math.sin(a)*r*.55);ctx.arc(x+Math.cos(a)*r*.55,y+Math.sin(a)*r*.55,r*.5,0,TAU);}
    ctx.moveTo(x+r*.7,y);ctx.arc(x,y,r*.7,0,TAU);
    ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.fill();
    ctx.lineWidth=lw;ctx.strokeStyle=line;ctx.stroke();
  }
  function starShape(ctx,x,y,r,rot,pts,inner){
    ctx.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<pts*2;i++){const a=rot-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/pts,rr=i%2?r*inner:r;ctx.lineTo(x+Math.cos(a)*rr,y+Math.sin(a)*rr);}ctx.closePath();
  }
  function drawFightCloud(ctx,tm,w,h,o){
    const s=clamp(o.strength/1.9,.4,1.5),R=Math.min(w,h)*.26*clamp(.7+o.scale*.3,.6,1.6),cx=w*.5,cy=h*.52;
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(cx,cy);
    const jx=Math.sin(tm*22)*R*.035,jy=Math.cos(tm*27)*R*.03;ctx.translate(jx,jy);
    ctx.lineJoin='round';
    const line='rgba(52,50,64,'+clamp(.95*s,.5,1)+')',lw=Math.max(2,R*.045);
    const layers=[[1,.55,1.0,[212,216,228]],[.7,.44,-1.25,[236,238,246]],[.38,.36,.9,[252,252,255]]];
    layers.forEach((L,li)=>{
      const cnt=li===0?10:li===1?8:5,rad=R*L[0];
      for(let i=0;i<cnt;i++){
        const a=i*TAU/cnt+tm*L[2]*1.6,wob=1+.1*Math.sin(tm*9+i*2.1+li),pr=R*L[1]*wob;
        const x=Math.cos(a)*rad,y=Math.sin(a)*rad*.9;
        cartoonPuff(ctx,x,y,pr,rgba(L[3],.96*clamp(s,.6,1)),line,lw);
      }
    });
    // pop-out stars, little impact lines and comic fists flying through the cloud
    ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
    for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
      const p=fr(tm*.9+i/7),a=hs(i+3)*TAU+Math.floor(tm*.9+i/7)*1.3,d=R*(.5+1.1*p),sz=R*(.16+.13*hs(i+8))*(1-p*.4);
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,214,40,'+(Math.sin(p*Math.PI)*.98)+')';ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(176,92,8,'+(Math.sin(p*Math.PI))+')';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,R*.03);
      starShape(ctx,Math.cos(a)*d,Math.sin(a)*d*.9,sz,tm*3+i,5,.46);ctx.fill();ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.lineCap='round';
    for(let i=0;i<9;i++){
      const a=i*TAU/9+Math.sin(tm*8)*.2,r0=R*(1.45+.12*Math.sin(tm*14+i)),r1=r0+R*.28;
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(60,56,76,'+(.7*clamp(s,.5,1))+')';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,R*.045);
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(a)*r0,Math.sin(a)*r0);ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*r1,Math.sin(a)*r1);ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();ctx.restore();
  }
  function drawSmokeRing(ctx,tm,w,h,o){
    const s=clamp(o.strength/1.9,.4,1.5),cx=w*.5,cy=h*.58,maxR=Math.min(w,h)*.48*clamp(.7+o.scale*.3,.6,1.5),squash=.55;
    ctx.save();ctx.lineJoin='round';
    for(let wave=0;wave<2;wave++){
      const p=fr(tm*.42+wave*.5),e=1-Math.pow(1-p,2.2),R=maxR*(.12+.88*e),alpha=Math.pow(1-p,.8);
      const cnt=16;
      for(let i=0;i<cnt;i++){
        const a=i*TAU/cnt+wave*.2,x=cx+Math.cos(a)*R,y=cy+Math.sin(a)*R*squash,pr=maxR*(.08+.1*p)*(.85+.3*hs(i+wave*9));
        ctx.globalAlpha=clamp(alpha*s,0,1);
        cartoonPuff(ctx,x,y,pr,'rgba(240,242,248,.96)','rgba(70,68,86,.95)',Math.max(1.8,maxR*.012));
      }
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha=1;
    // dust kicked up in the centre
    const p0=fr(tm*.42);
    const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(cx,cy,1,cx,cy,maxR*.5);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,255,255,'+clamp(.5*(1-p0)*s,0,.7)+')');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(cx,cy,maxR*.5,maxR*.5*squash,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function drawImpact(ctx,tm,w,h,o){
    const s=clamp(o.strength/1.9,.4,1.5),R=Math.min(w,h)*.3*clamp(.7+o.scale*.3,.6,1.6),cx=w*.5,cy=h*.5,cyc=1.15,ph=fr(tm/cyc),pop=ph<.14?ease(ph/.14)*1.22:ph<.3?lerp(1.22,1,ease((ph-.14)/.16)):1+.03*Math.sin(tm*14);
    const fade=ph>.82?1-(ph-.82)/.18:1;
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(cx,cy);ctx.globalAlpha=clamp(fade,0,1);
    // radial speed streaks
    ctx.lineCap='round';
    for(let i=0;i<28;i++){
      const a=i*TAU/28+hs(i)*.1,r0=R*(1.25+.3*hs(i+4)),r1=r0+R*(.55+.6*hs(i+9))*clamp(ph*4,.2,1);
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,'+(.35+.4*hs(i+2))*clamp(s,.5,1)+')';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.2,R*.02*(1+hs(i+6)));
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(a)*r0,Math.sin(a)*r0);ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*r1,Math.sin(a)*r1);ctx.stroke();
    }
    // expanding shock rings
    for(let k=0;k<2;k++){
      const q=clamp(ph*1.6-k*.18,0,1);if(q<=0||q>=1)continue;
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,246,200,'+((1-q)*.9)+')';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,R*.06*(1-q));ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,R*(1+q*1.35),0,TAU);ctx.stroke();
    }
    // comic starburst
    ctx.save();ctx.scale(pop,pop);ctx.rotate(Math.sin(tm*5)*.04);ctx.lineJoin='miter';
    const spikes=15;
    const path=(rr,inn)=>{ctx.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<spikes*2;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/spikes,r=(i%2?rr*inn:rr)*(i%2?1:(.9+.18*hs(i)));ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r);}ctx.closePath();};
    path(R,.58);ctx.fillStyle='#3A0E10';ctx.fill();
    path(R*.94,.58);let g=ctx.createRadialGradient(0,0,R*.1,0,0,R);g.addColorStop(0,'#FFF6B0');g.addColorStop(.45,'#FFC21A');g.addColorStop(1,'#FF5A12');ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fill();
    ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,R*.045);ctx.strokeStyle='#7A1A0C';ctx.stroke();
    path(R*.55,.62);ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,235,.92)';ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function drawSpeedLines(ctx,tm,w,h,o){
    const s=clamp(o.strength/1.9,.4,1.5),cx=w*.5,cy=h*.5,far=Math.hypot(w,h)*.55,n=Math.round(46*clamp(o.density/1.35,.5,2));
    ctx.save();ctx.translate(cx,cy);ctx.lineCap='round';
    const vg=ctx.createRadialGradient(0,0,far*.2,0,0,far);vg.addColorStop(0,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');vg.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,'+clamp(.28*s,.08,.5)+')');ctx.fillStyle=vg;ctx.fillRect(-w,-h,w*2,h*2);
    const step=Math.floor(tm*16);
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
      const hv=hs(i*1.7+step*.37),a=(i/n)*TAU+hs(i+step)*.07,r0=far*(.34+.3*hs(i+11)+.06*hv),len=far*(.22+.38*hs(i+5+step*.1));
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,'+(.55+.4*hs(i+8))*clamp(s,.5,1)+')';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.2,w*.004*(.6+hs(i+3)*1.6));
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(a)*r0,Math.sin(a)*r0);ctx.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*(r0+len),Math.sin(a)*(r0+len));ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function drawShockwave(ctx,tm,w,h,o){
    const s=clamp(o.strength/1.9,.4,1.5),cx=w*.5,cy=h*.5,maxR=Math.min(w,h)*.55*clamp(.7+o.scale*.3,.6,1.5);
    ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';
    for(let k=0;k<3;k++){
      const p=fr(tm*.55+k/3),e=1-Math.pow(1-p,2.4),R=maxR*(.06+.94*e),a=Math.pow(1-p,1.4)*clamp(s,.5,1.3);
      const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(cx,cy,R*.78,cx,cy,R*1.06);
      g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(120,200,255,0)');g.addColorStop(.62,'rgba(190,230,255,'+(a*.55)+')');g.addColorStop(.82,'rgba(255,255,255,'+(a*.95)+')');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(cx,cy,R*1.06,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
    }
    const p0=fr(tm*.55),fl=Math.pow(1-p0,3)*.55*s;
    const core=ctx.createRadialGradient(cx,cy,1,cx,cy,maxR*.45);core.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,255,255,'+fl+')');core.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle=core;ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function draw(ctx,type,tm,w,h,o){
    try{
      if(FIRE_PAL[type])return drawFire(ctx,type,tm,w,h,o);
      if(type==='waves'||type==='wavesurge')return drawWaves(ctx,type,tm,w,h,o);
      if(SMOKE_PAL[type])return drawSmoke(ctx,type,tm,w,h,o);
      if(type==='fightcloud')return drawFightCloud(ctx,tm,w,h,o);
      if(type==='smokering')return drawSmokeRing(ctx,tm,w,h,o);
      if(type==='impactburst')return drawImpact(ctx,tm,w,h,o);
      if(type==='speedlines')return drawSpeedLines(ctx,tm,w,h,o);
      if(type==='shockwave')return drawShockwave(ctx,tm,w,h,o);
    }catch(e){try{console.warn('Ethvelle scene effect',type,e);}catch(_e){}}
  }

  /* little line icons for timeline strips */
  const ICON={
    fire:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 2c1 4 5 6 5 11a5 5 0 0 1-10 0c0-2 1-3.5 2-4.5 0 2 1 3 2 3 0-4-1-6 1-9.5Z"/></svg>',
    water:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M2 9c2.5-3 4.5-3 7 0s4.5 3 7 0 4.5-3 6 0M2 15c2.5-3 4.5-3 7 0s4.5 3 7 0 4.5-3 6 0"/></svg>',
    smoke:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 19a4 4 0 0 1-.5-8A5.5 5.5 0 0 1 16 9a4 4 0 0 1 2 10H6Z"/><path d="M9 22h8"/></svg>',
    fight:'<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m12 2 2.2 5.2L19.5 5l-1.6 5.5L23 12l-5.1 1.5 1.6 5.5-5.3-2.2L12 22l-2.2-5.2L4.5 19l1.6-5.5L1 12l5.1-1.5L4.5 5l5.3 2.2Z"/></svg>'
  };
  const GROUP={};TYPES.forEach(t=>GROUP[t[0]]=t[2]);

  window.__evSceneFxPack={
    types:TYPES,labels:LABEL,glyphs:GLYPH,
    has:t=>!!GROUP[t],
    draw,
    icon:t=>GROUP[t]?ICON[GROUP[t]]:'',
    groups:[['Fire','fire'],['Water','water'],['Smoke','smoke'],['Fighting','fight']]
  };
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v70-visual-toolkit-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const clamp=(v,min,max)=>Math.max(min,Math.min(max,Number(v)||0));
  const isCharClip=()=>selectedClip==='main'||(typeof selectedClip==='string'&&String(selectedClip).startsWith('seg'));
  const isAudio=()=>Array.isArray(overlays)&&overlays.some(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(selectedClip)&&o.type==='audio');
  const bgSelected=()=>{try{return typeof backgroundClipForSelection==='function'?backgroundClipForSelection():(Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds.find(b=>b&&b.id===selectedClip):null);}catch(_e){return null;}};
  function visualTarget(){
    if(isAudio())return null;
    const bg=bgSelected();if(bg)return {kind:'background',obj:bg,transform:bg.transform||(bg.transform={scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0})};
    if(isCharClip()||String(selectedClip||'').startsWith('char')){
      let idx=activeCharIdx;if(String(selectedClip||'').startsWith('char')){const n=parseInt(String(selectedClip).slice(4),10);if(Number.isInteger(n))idx=n;}
      const ch=Array.isArray(characters)?characters[idx]:null;return ch?{kind:'character',obj:ch,transform:ch.modelTransform||{}}:null;
    }
    const ov=Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(selectedClip)&&['image','video','text','shape','color'].includes(o.type)):null;
    return ov?{kind:'layer',obj:ov,transform:ov.transform||(ov.transform={scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0})}:null;
  }
  function ensureContextTools(){
    const row=$('bottomToolsClip');if(!row)return;
    const before=$('clipForwardTool')||$('clipDuplicateTool')||null;
    const make=(id,label,icon,path)=>{
      let b=$(id);if(b)return b;
      b=document.createElement('div');b.className='tool-item evv-context-tool';b.id=id;b.setAttribute('role','button');b.tabIndex=0;
      b.innerHTML=`<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">${path}</svg><span>${label}</span>`;
      b.addEventListener('click',()=>window.openPanel(id==='clipMirrorTool'?'visualMirror':id==='clipMaskTool'?'visualMask':'visualEffects'));
      b.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();b.click();}});
      row.insertBefore(b,before);return b;
    };
    make('clipMirrorTool','Mirror','mirror','<path d="M12 3v18M4 6h5v12H4zM15 6h5v12h-5z"/>');
    make('clipMaskTool','Mask','mask','<path d="M4 4h16v16H4z"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="5"/><path d="M4 12h3m10 0h3"/>');
    make('clipEffectsTool','Effects','fx','<path d="M12 2l1.8 6.2L20 10l-6.2 1.8L12 18l-1.8-6.2L4 10l6.2-1.8z"/><path d="M19 16l.8 2.2L22 19l-2.2.8L19 22l-.8-2.2L17 19z"/>');
  }
  function syncContextTools(){
    ensureContextTools();const target=visualTarget();
    ['clipMirrorTool','clipMaskTool','clipEffectsTool'].forEach(id=>{const el=$(id);if(el)el.style.display=target?'flex':'none';});
    const fx=$('clipEffectsTool');if(fx)fx.querySelector('span').textContent='Effects';
    const zoom=$('transportZoomBtn');if(zoom){zoom.style.display='inline-flex';zoom.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(inlineZoom));zoom.title=inlineZoom?'Exit canvas zoom':'Zoom canvas';}
  }
  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__evv70){const wrapped=function(){const r=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);syncContextTools();return r;};wrapped.__evv70=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;}

  /* ---------- Persistent mirror and mask state ---------- */
  function getMask(obj){
    if(!obj)return {shape:'none',x:0,y:0,width:70,height:70,rotation:0,feather:0,invert:false};
    obj.visualMask=Object.assign({shape:'none',x:0,y:0,width:70,height:70,size:100,rotation:0,feather:0,invert:false},obj.visualMask||{});
    return obj.visualMask;
  }
  function mirrorStore(t){return t&&t.kind==='background'?t.transform:(t&&t.obj)||{};}
  function targetMirror(t){const obj=mirrorStore(t);return {x:!!obj.mirrorX,y:!!obj.mirrorY};}
  window.setEthvelleMirror=function(axis){const t=visualTarget();if(!t)return;const key=axis==='y'?'mirrorY':'mirrorX',store=mirrorStore(t);store[key]=!store[key];if(t.kind==='background')t.obj[key]=store[key];refreshVisual();renderVisualPanel('visualMirror');};
  window.resetEthvelleMirror=function(){const t=visualTarget();if(!t)return;const store=mirrorStore(t);store.mirrorX=false;store.mirrorY=false;if(t.kind==='background'){t.obj.mirrorX=false;t.obj.mirrorY=false;}refreshVisual();renderVisualPanel('visualMirror');};
  function pathData(mask,w,h){
    const cx=w*(.5+clamp(mask.x,-45,45)/100),cy=h*(.5+clamp(mask.y,-45,45)/100),scale=clamp(mask.size==null?100:mask.size,25,140)/100,mw=w*clamp(mask.width*scale,5,100)/100,mh=h*clamp(mask.height*scale,5,100)/100;
    const l=cx-mw/2,r=cx+mw/2,t=cy-mh/2,b=cy+mh/2,shape=mask.shape||'none';
    let d='';
    if(shape==='circle')d=`M ${cx} ${t} A ${mw/2} ${mh/2} 0 1 1 ${cx-.001} ${t} Z`;
    else if(shape==='rectangle')d=`M ${l} ${t} H ${r} V ${b} H ${l} Z`;
    else if(shape==='linear')d=`M ${cx} ${cy-mh} L ${cx+mw} ${cy+mh} L ${cx+mw} ${cy+mh*1.2} L ${cx-mw} ${cy-mh*1.2} Z`;
    else if(shape==='mirror')d=`M ${l} ${t} H ${r} V ${b} H ${l} Z`;
    else if(shape==='heart'){
      const top=cy-mh*.26;
      d=`M ${cx} ${b} C ${l-mw*.08} ${cy+mh*.18}, ${l} ${top}, ${cx-mw*.25} ${top} C ${cx-mw*.08} ${cy-mh*.62}, ${cx} ${cy-mh*.28}, ${cx} ${cy-mh*.05} C ${cx} ${cy-mh*.28}, ${cx+mw*.08} ${cy-mh*.62}, ${cx+mw*.25} ${top} C ${r} ${top}, ${r+mw*.08} ${cy+mh*.18}, ${cx} ${b} Z`;
    }else if(shape==='star'){
      const pts=[];for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const a=-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/5,rr=i%2?0.23:0.5;pts.push([cx+Math.cos(a)*mw*rr,cy+Math.sin(a)*mh*rr]);}d=pts.map((p,i)=>(i?'L':'M')+' '+p[0]+' '+p[1]).join(' ')+' Z';
    }
    if(!d)return '';
    const rot=clamp(mask.rotation,-180,180);return `<path d="${d}" transform="rotate(${rot} ${cx} ${cy})"/>`;
  }
  function pathDataSvg(mask){
    const scale=clamp(mask.size==null?100:mask.size,25,140)/100,x=500+clamp(mask.x,-45,45)*10,y=500+clamp(mask.y,-45,45)*10,w=clamp(mask.width*scale,5,100)*10,h=clamp(mask.height*scale,5,100)*10,l=x-w/2,r=x+w/2,t=y-h/2,b=y+h/2;
    let d='';
    if(mask.shape==='circle')d=`M ${x} ${t} A ${w/2} ${h/2} 0 1 1 ${x-.01} ${t} Z`;
    else if(mask.shape==='rectangle'||mask.shape==='mirror')d=`M ${l} ${t} H ${r} V ${b} H ${l} Z`;
    else if(mask.shape==='linear')d=`M ${x} 0 L 1000 1000 L 1000 1000 L 0 1000 Z`;
    else if(mask.shape==='heart')d=`M ${x} ${b} C ${l-24} ${y+28}, ${l} ${y-15}, ${x-w*.25} ${y-h*.12} C ${x-w*.08} ${y-h*.6}, ${x} ${y-h*.25}, ${x} ${y-h*.06} C ${x} ${y-h*.25}, ${x+w*.08} ${y-h*.6}, ${x+w*.25} ${y-h*.12} C ${r} ${y-15}, ${r+24} ${y+28}, ${x} ${b} Z`;
    else if(mask.shape==='star'){const pts=[];for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const a=-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/5,rr=i%2?.23:.5;pts.push([x+Math.cos(a)*w*rr,y+Math.sin(a)*h*rr]);}d=pts.map((p,i)=>(i?'L':'M')+' '+p[0]+' '+p[1]).join(' ')+' Z';}
    return d?`<path d="${d}" transform="rotate(${clamp(mask.rotation,-180,180)} ${x} ${y})"/>`:'';
  }
  window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip=function(ctx,mask,w,h){
    if(!ctx||!mask||!mask.shape||mask.shape==='none'||typeof Path2D==='undefined')return;
    try{
      const pData=pathData(mask,w,h);if(!pData)return;
      const d=pData.match(/d="([^"]+)"/);if(!d)return;
      const path=new Path2D(d[1]);
      if(mask.invert){const outer=new Path2D();outer.rect(0,0,w,h);outer.addPath(path);ctx.clip(outer,'evenodd');}
      else ctx.clip(path);
    }catch(e){console.warn('Ethvelle canvas mask skipped:',e);}
  };
  function svgMaskUrl(mask){
    if(!mask||!mask.shape||mask.shape==='none')return '';
    const path=pathDataSvg(mask);if(!path)return '';
    const invert=!!mask.invert,feather=clamp(mask.feather,0,50)*.55;
    const filter=feather>0?`<filter id="f" x="-20%" y="-20%" width="140%" height="140%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="${feather.toFixed(1)}"/></filter>`:'';
    const fg=invert?'black':'white',bg=invert?'white':'black';
    const svg=`<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="1000" height="1000" viewBox="0 0 1000 1000"><defs>${filter}<mask id="m" maskUnits="userSpaceOnUse" x="0" y="0" width="1000" height="1000"><rect width="1000" height="1000" fill="${bg}"/><g ${feather>0?'filter="url(#f)"':''}>${path.replace(/<path/,'<path fill="'+fg+'"')}</g></mask></defs><rect width="1000" height="1000" fill="white" mask="url(#m)"/></svg>`;
    return 'url("data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(svg)+'")';
  }
  function applyDomMask(el,mask){
    if(!el||!el.style)return;
    const signature=mask?JSON.stringify(mask):'none';
    if(el.dataset&&el.dataset.evDomMaskSignature===signature)return;
    const url=svgMaskUrl(mask);
    el.style.webkitMaskImage=url||'none';el.style.maskImage=url||'none';el.style.webkitMaskSize='100% 100%';el.style.maskSize='100% 100%';el.style.webkitMaskRepeat='no-repeat';el.style.maskRepeat='no-repeat';el.style.webkitMaskMode='luminance';el.style.maskMode='luminance';
    if(el.dataset)el.dataset.evDomMaskSignature=signature;
  }
  function applyOverlayVisualStyle(el,clip,tSec,referenceEl,isBackground){
    if(!el||!el.style||!clip)return el;
    const frameTime=Number.isFinite(Number(tSec))?Number(tSec):0;
    const reference=referenceEl&&referenceEl.style?referenceEl:el;
    const keyed=typeof ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(clip,'transform',frameTime):null;
    const isClipTracked=!!(!isBackground&&clip.clipTracking&&clip.clipTracking.enabled&&clip.clipTracking.targetId);
    const hasLegacyTracking=!!(!isBackground&&clip.characterTracking&&clip.characterTracking.enabled);
    // Tracking interpolation is among the costliest per-frame visual operations. Do not call
    // it for every ordinary overlay; only tracked clips (including legacy character tracking)
    // need a source transform lookup.
    const tracked=(isClipTracked||hasLegacyTracking)&&!isBackground&&window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform
      ?window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(clip,frameTime,keyed):null;
    const tr=(isClipTracked?(tracked||keyed):(keyed||tracked))||clip.transform||(isBackground?bgTransform:null)||{};
    let transform='';
    if(isBackground){
      transform=`translate(${Number(tr.offsetX)||0}px, ${Number(tr.offsetY)||0}px) rotate(${Number(tr.rotation)||0}deg) scale(${Number(tr.scale)||1}) scaleX(${tr.mirrorX?-1:1}) scaleY(${tr.mirrorY?-1:1})`;
    }else{
      const motion=window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState?window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(clip,frameTime):{x:0,y:0,rotation:0,scale:1,opacity:1};
      transform=`translate(${(Number(tr.offsetX)||0)+(Number(motion.x)||0)}px, ${(Number(tr.offsetY)||0)+(Number(motion.y)||0)}px) rotate(${(Number(tr.rotation)||0)+(Number(motion.rotation)||0)}deg) scale(${(Number(tr.scale)||1)*(Number(motion.scale)||1)}) scaleX(${clip.mirrorX?-1:1}) scaleY(${clip.mirrorY?-1:1})`;
    }
    transform+=(window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix?window.ethvellePerspectiveSuffix(clip):'');
    if(el.style.transform!==transform)el.style.transform=transform;
    if(el.style.transformOrigin!=='center center'&&!reference.style.transformOrigin)el.style.transformOrigin='center center';
    if(el.dataset&&clip.id!=null)el.dataset.evMaskOwnerId=String(clip.id);
    if(reference!==el){
      // Stand-ins are styled from a cached source-style snapshot. Reading computed style on
      // every playback frame forced repeated style/layout work on Android for each canvas.
      const styleProps=['filter','clipPath','webkitClipPath','objectFit','objectPosition','borderRadius','transformOrigin'];
      const sourceSig=styleProps.map(prop=>reference.style[prop]||'').join('|');
      let cached=reference.__evVisualStandInStyleCache;
      if(!cached||cached.signature!==sourceSig){
        let computed=null;try{computed=getComputedStyle(reference);}catch(_e){}
        const value=(prop,fallback)=>reference.style[prop]||(computed&&computed[prop])||fallback;
        cached={signature:sourceSig,filter:value('filter','none'),clipPath:value('clipPath','none'),webkitClipPath:value('webkitClipPath',value('clipPath','none')),objectFit:value('objectFit','contain'),objectPosition:value('objectPosition','50% 50%'),borderRadius:value('borderRadius','0px'),transformOrigin:value('transformOrigin','center center')};
        reference.__evVisualStandInStyleCache=cached;
      }
      styleProps.forEach(prop=>{const value=cached[prop];if(value!=null&&el.style[prop]!==String(value))el.style[prop]=String(value);});
    }
    applyDomMask(el,clip.visualMask);
    // Blur is a true per-layer media filter, not a full-stage canvas wash. Keep each
    // element's pre-existing enhancement/filter string and only append/remove our own blur.
    try{
      const fx=window.ethvelleGetActiveClipBlurEffect?window.ethvelleGetActiveClipBlurEffect(clip,frameTime):(clip.visualEffects&&clip.visualEffects.type==='blur'?clip.visualEffects:null);
      const current=String(el.style.filter||'');
      const previous=el.__evEthvelleFxFilter;
      if(previous==null||current!==previous)el.__evEthvelleFxBaseFilter=current||'none';
      if(fx){
        const radius=Math.max(0,Math.min(14,(Number(fx.intensity)||135)/30));
        const base=String(el.__evEthvelleFxBaseFilter||'none');
        const next=(base!=='none'?base+' ':'')+'blur('+radius.toFixed(2)+'px)';
        if(el.style.filter!==next)el.style.filter=next;
        el.__evEthvelleFxFilter=next;
      }else if(previous!=null){
        if(current===previous)el.style.filter=el.__evEthvelleFxBaseFilter||'none';
        delete el.__evEthvelleFxFilter;delete el.__evEthvelleFxBaseFilter;
      }
    }catch(_e){}
    return el;
  }
  window.ethvelleApplyOverlayVisualStyle=applyOverlayVisualStyle;
  function syncBgVisual(){
    const img=$('bgImgLayer');if(!img)return;
    const frameTime=typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?Number(getCurrentAbsoluteTime()):Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    // Draw the background that covers the playhead. The selected background remains the
    // editing target, but must not replace another sequential background outside its range.
    let bg=typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'?currentBackgroundClip(frameTime):null;
    if(!bg)bg=bgSelected();
    if(!bg)return;
    // Keep the final visual-sync wrapper on the same interpolated value as applyBackground;
    // otherwise this later pass would overwrite the keyframe-aware transform with the base.
    applyOverlayVisualStyle(img,bg,frameTime,img,true);
  }
  function syncOverlayVisuals(tSec){
    if(!Array.isArray(overlays))return;
    const frameTime=Number(tSec)||0;
    const active=overlays.filter(o=>o&&['image','video','text','shape','color'].includes(o.type)&&frameTime>=Number(o.trimStart||0)&&frameTime<Number(o.trimEnd||0));
    active.forEach(ov=>{
      const el=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls[ov.id])||null;
      if(el)applyOverlayVisualStyle(el,ov,frameTime,el,false);
      const chroma=(typeof overlayChromaCanvases!=='undefined'&&overlayChromaCanvases[ov.id])||null;
      if(chroma)applyOverlayVisualStyle(chroma,ov,frameTime,el||chroma,false);
    });
  }
  const oldBgApply=window.applyBgTransform;
  if(typeof oldBgApply==='function'&&!oldBgApply.__evv70){const wrapped=function(){const r=oldBgApply.apply(this,arguments);syncBgVisual();return r;};wrapped.__evv70=true;window.applyBgTransform=wrapped;}
  const oldOverlayRender=window.renderOverlayMedia;
  if(typeof oldOverlayRender==='function'&&!oldOverlayRender.__evv70){
    // The core compositor already styles every active overlay. A second full scan here used
    // to repeat keyframe/track lookups and mask work on every playback tick, hurting Android FPS.
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldOverlayRender.apply(this,arguments);syncBgVisual();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv70=true;window.renderOverlayMedia=wrapped;
  }

  /* ---------- Visual tool panels ---------- */
  try{
    panelContent.visualMirror={title:'Mirror',body:'<div id="evvVisualPanel"></div>'};
    panelContent.visualMask={title:'Mask',body:'<div id="evvVisualPanel"></div>'};
    panelContent.visualEffects={title:'Effects',body:'<div id="evvVisualPanel"></div>'};
  }catch(_e){}
  function sliderHtml(prop,label,value,min,max,step,unit){const id='evv_'+prop;return `<div class="evv-slider-card"><div class="evv-slider-head"><label for="${id}">${label}</label><output id="${id}_out">${Math.round(Number(value)||0)}${unit||''}</output></div><input id="${id}" type="range" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step}" value="${value}" oninput="setEthvelleMaskValue('${prop}',this.value,false);document.getElementById('${id}_out').textContent=(Math.round(Number(this.value)*10)/10)+'${unit||''}'" onchange="setEthvelleMaskValue('${prop}',this.value,true)"></div>`;}
  function fxSliderHtml(prop,label,value,min,max,step,unit){const id='evv_fx_'+prop;const v=Number(value)||0;return `<div class="evv-slider-card evv-fx-slider-card"><div class="evv-slider-head"><label for="${id}">${label}</label><output id="${id}_out">${Math.round(v)}${unit||''}</output></div><input id="${id}" type="range" min="${min}" max="${max}" step="${step}" value="${v}" oninput="setEthvelleEffectSetting('${prop}',this.value,false);document.getElementById('${id}_out').textContent=(Math.round(Number(this.value)*10)/10)+'${unit||''}'" onchange="setEthvelleEffectSetting('${prop}',this.value,true)"></div>`;}
  function visualChoice(value,label,active,fn){return `<button type="button" class="evv-choice ${active?'active':''}" onclick="${fn}">${label}</button>`;}
  const maskShapes=[['none','None'],['linear','Linear'],['mirror','Mirror Band'],['circle','Circle'],['rectangle','Rectangle'],['heart','Heart'],['star','Star']];
  const charFx=[['none','None'],['anger','Anger'],['fire','Fire'],['tears','Tears'],['chi','Chi Ball'],['blood','Blood'],['bandage','Bandage'],['bruise','Black Eye'],['nosebleed','Nosebleed'],['sweat','Sweat'],['dizzy','Dizzy Stars'],['hearts','Hearts'],['sleep','Sleeping'],['electric','Electric Shock'],['sparkle','Sparkles']];
  const sceneFx=[['none','None']]; /* v435: inbuilt scene effects removed */
  window.renderVisualPanel=function(key){renderVisualPanel(key);};
  function renderVisualPanel(key){
    const host=$('evvVisualPanel'),t=visualTarget();if(!host||!t){if(host)host.innerHTML='';return;}
    if(key==='visualMirror'){
      const m=targetMirror(t);
      host.innerHTML=`<div class="evv-panel"><div class="evv-panel-title">Mirror</div><div class="evv-choice-grid">${visualChoice('x','Horizontal',m.x,"setEthvelleMirror('x')")}${visualChoice('y','Vertical',m.y,"setEthvelleMirror('y')")}${visualChoice('reset','Reset',!m.x&&!m.y,'resetEthvelleMirror()')}</div></div>`;return;
    }
    if(key==='visualMask'){
      const m=getMask(t.obj);
      host.innerHTML=`<div class="evv-panel"><div class="evv-panel-title">Mask</div><div class="evv-choice-grid">${maskShapes.map(([v,l])=>visualChoice(v,l,m.shape===v,`setEthvelleMaskValue('shape','${v}',true)`)).join('')}</div><label class="evv-switch-row"><span>Invert</span><input type="checkbox" ${m.invert?'checked':''} onchange="setEthvelleMaskValue('invert',this.checked,true)"></label>${sliderHtml('size','Size',m.size==null?100:m.size,25,140,1,'%')}${sliderHtml('width','Width',m.width,5,100,1,'%')}${sliderHtml('height','Height',m.height,5,100,1,'%')}${sliderHtml('x','Horizontal Position',m.x,-45,45,1,'%')}${sliderHtml('y','Vertical Position',m.y,-45,45,1,'%')}${sliderHtml('rotation','Rotation',m.rotation,-180,180,1,'°')}${t.kind!=='character'?sliderHtml('feather','Feather',m.feather,0,50,1,'%'):''}<button type="button" class="evv-choice" style="aspect-ratio:auto;min-height:40px" onclick="setEthvelleMaskValue('shape','none',true)">Remove Mask</button></div>`;
      wireVisualRanges(host);return;
    }
    if(key==='visualEffects'){
      const isCharClip=t.kind==='layer'&&t.obj&&t.obj.ethvelleEffectKind==='character',isChar=t.kind==='character'||isCharClip,store=t.obj.visualEffects||(t.obj.visualEffects={type:'none'}),items=isCharClip?charFx.filter(x=>x[0]!=='none'):(isChar?charFx:sceneFx);
      if(isChar){host.innerHTML='';return;} /* old built-in Character Effects section removed: character effects now come from the admin Effects library */
      const groups=typeof window.__evFxGroups==='function'?window.__evFxGroups(isChar,isCharClip,items):[[null,items]];
      host.innerHTML=`<div class="evv-panel">${groups.map(([title,list])=>(title?`<div class="evv-fx-group-title">${title}</div>`:'')+`<div class="evv-choice-grid">${list.map(([v,l])=>visualChoice(v,l,store.type===v,`setEthvelleVisualEffect('${v}')`)).join('')}</div>`).join('')}</div>`;return;
    }
  }
  function wireVisualRanges(host){host.querySelectorAll('input[type=range]').forEach(input=>{const paint=()=>{const min=Number(input.min)||0,max=Number(input.max)||100,v=Number(input.value)||0;input.style.setProperty('--evv-progress',clamp((v-min)/(max-min||1)*100,0,100)+'%');};input.addEventListener('input',paint);paint();});}
  window.setEthvelleMaskValue=function(prop,value,commit){const t=visualTarget();if(!t)return;const m=getMask(t.obj);
    if(prop==='shape'){m.shape=maskShapes.some(x=>x[0]===String(value))?String(value):'none';}
    else if(prop==='invert')m.invert=!!value;
    else if(prop==='size')m.size=clamp(value,25,140);
    else if(['width','height'].includes(prop))m[prop]=clamp(value,5,100);
    else if(['x','y'].includes(prop))m[prop]=clamp(value,-45,45);
    else if(prop==='rotation')m.rotation=clamp(value,-180,180);
    else if(prop==='feather')m.feather=clamp(value,0,50);
    refreshVisual();if(commit){persistVisual();renderVisualPanel('visualMask');}
  };
  const fxDefaults={type:'none',intensity:135,density:135,speed:100,scale:100,x:0,y:0};
  function ensureFxSettings(obj){obj.visualEffects=Object.assign({},fxDefaults,obj.visualEffects||{});return obj.visualEffects;}
  window.setEthvelleVisualEffect=function(type){const t=visualTarget();if(!t)return;const isChar=t.kind==='character',items=isChar?charFx:sceneFx;if(!items.some(x=>x[0]===type))return;const fx=ensureFxSettings(t.obj);fx.type=type;refreshVisual();persistVisual();renderVisualPanel('visualEffects');};
  window.setEthvelleEffectSetting=function(prop,value,commit){const t=visualTarget();if(!t)return;const fx=ensureFxSettings(t.obj);const limits={intensity:[0,250],density:[10,300],speed:[0,300],scale:[25,250],quality:[25,150],x:[-100,100],y:[-100,100]};if(!limits[prop])return;fx[prop]=clamp(value,limits[prop][0],limits[prop][1]);
    // Slider drags repaint only the effect canvas. Avoid a full project render and media
    // compositor pass for every pointer event; persist once on release.
    if(commit){try{if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();else if(typeof drawFxPreview==='function')drawFxPreview();}catch(_e){}persistVisual();}
    else{try{if(typeof drawFxPreview==='function')drawFxPreview();else if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();}catch(_e){}}
  };
  window.setEthvelleEffectIntensity=function(value){window.setEthvelleEffectSetting('intensity',value,false);};
  // slider setter also updates the selected FX intensity field.
  const maskSetter=window.setEthvelleMaskValue;
  window.setEthvelleMaskValue=function(prop,value,commit){if(prop==='intensity'){window.setEthvelleEffectIntensity(value);if(commit)renderVisualPanel('visualEffects');return;}return maskSetter(prop,value,commit);};
  function persistVisual(){try{if(typeof persistProjectState==='function')persistProjectState();}catch(e){console.warn('Visual setting save:',e);}}
  function refreshVisual(){
    try{
      // renderFrame owns the stage/compositor refresh in normal editor use. Avoid separately
      // walking and styling every overlay a second time after the frame has already rendered.
      if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();
      else if(typeof drawStick==='function')drawStick();
      if(typeof applyBgTransform==='function')applyBgTransform();
    }catch(e){console.warn('Visual refresh:',e);}
  }
  const openPanelBase=window.openPanel;
  if(typeof openPanelBase==='function'&&!openPanelBase.__evv70){const wrapped=function(key){const r=openPanelBase.apply(this,arguments);if(['visualMirror','visualMask','visualEffects'].includes(key))requestAnimationFrame(()=>renderVisualPanel(key));return r;};wrapped.__evv70=true;window.openPanel=wrapped;}

  /* ---------- Per-character and per-layer animated effects ---------- */
  function boneEndpoints(char,key){
    const bs=char&&char.bones||{};if(!bs[key])return null;
    function start(k,guard){const b=bs[k];if(!b)return {x:0,y:0};if(!b.parent)return {x:b.x||0,y:b.y||0};const e=end(k,(guard||0)+1);if(b.attachAt!==undefined){const p=start(b.parent,(guard||0)+1);const pe=end(b.parent,(guard||0)+1);return {x:p.x+(pe.x-p.x)*b.attachAt,y:p.y+(pe.y-p.y)*b.attachAt};}return e;}
    function end(k,guard){if(guard>20)return {x:0,y:0};const b=bs[k];if(!b)return {x:0,y:0};const st=startNoRecurse(k,guard);return {x:st.x+Math.cos(Number(b.angle)||0)*(Number(b.length)||0),y:st.y+Math.sin(Number(b.angle)||0)*(Number(b.length)||0)};}
    function startNoRecurse(k,guard){const b=bs[k];if(!b.parent)return {x:b.x||0,y:b.y||0};const pe=end(b.parent,guard+1);if(b.attachAt!==undefined){const ps=startNoRecurse(b.parent,guard+1);return {x:ps.x+(pe.x-ps.x)*b.attachAt,y:ps.y+(pe.y-ps.y)*b.attachAt};}return pe;}
    const st=startNoRecurse(key,0),ed=end(key,0);return {start:st,end:ed};
  }
  function charPoint(char,p){
    if(!p)return {x:stageW*.5,y:stageH*.35};const mt=char.modelTransform||{scale:1,offsetX:0,offsetY:0,rotation:0},hip=char.bones&&char.bones.hip||{x:stageW*.5,y:stageH*.55};
    let x=(p.x-hip.x)*(Number(mt.scale)||1),y=(p.y-hip.y)*(Number(mt.scale)||1);if(char.mirrorX)x=-x;if(char.mirrorY)y=-y;const a=(Number(mt.rotation)||0)*Math.PI/180;return {x:hip.x+(Number(mt.offsetX)||0)+x*Math.cos(a)-y*Math.sin(a),y:hip.y+(Number(mt.offsetY)||0)+x*Math.sin(a)+y*Math.cos(a)};
  }
  /* v411: face anchors. Eyes / cheeks / nose / temple are placed from the character's own head art
     (width : height of the head part when known, model type otherwise) instead of fixed numbers. */
  function faceGeom(char){
    let aspect=0.9;
    try{
      const parts=(char&&char.sheet&&char.sheet.parts)||(char&&char.parts)||{},hp=parts.head;
      if(hp){
        let w=Number(hp.w||hp.width||hp.naturalWidth),h=Number(hp.h||hp.height||hp.naturalHeight);
        const b=hp.contentBox;if(b&&b.w>0&&b.h>0){w=b.w;h=b.h;}
        if(w>0&&h>0)aspect=clamp(w/h,.45,1.8);
      }
    }catch(_e){}
    const mt=String((char&&(char.modelType||(char.sheet&&char.sheet.modelType)))||'humanoid');
    const snout=(mt==='animal'||mt==='bird');
    const fw=clamp(aspect/0.9,.72,1.38);
    return {fw:fw,ex:(snout?.34:.4)*fw,cx:(snout?.46:.52)*fw,ey:snout?.0:.08,ny:snout?.3:.34,my:snout?.46:.5,sideX:.92*fw};
  }
  /* v334: head frame (centre, radius, tilt, mirror, hand) used by every character effect. */
  function charFrame(char){
    if(!char||!char.bones||!char.bones.head)return null;
    const ha=boneEndpoints(char,'head');if(!ha)return null;
    const mt=char.modelTransform||{scale:1,rotation:0},sc=Number(mt.scale)||1;
    const mid=charPoint(char,{x:(ha.start.x+ha.end.x)/2,y:(ha.start.y+ha.end.y)/2});
    const len=Math.hypot(ha.end.x-ha.start.x,ha.end.y-ha.start.y)*sc;
    if(!isFinite(mid.x)||!isFinite(mid.y)||!(len>0))return null;
    let tilt=Math.atan2(ha.end.y-ha.start.y,ha.end.x-ha.start.x)+Math.PI/2,flip=false;
    if(char.mirrorX){tilt=-tilt;flip=!flip;}
    if(char.mirrorY){tilt=Math.PI-tilt;flip=!flip;}
    tilt+=(Number(mt.rotation)||0)*Math.PI/180;
    const arm=boneEndpoints(char,'armR_lo')||boneEndpoints(char,'armL_lo');
    const hand=arm?charPoint(char,arm.end):{x:mid.x+len*1.5,y:mid.y+len*1.6};
    return {x:mid.x,y:mid.y,r:Math.max(6,len*.55),rot:tilt,flip,hand,face:faceGeom(char)};
  }
  function charFxCfg(fx){
    fx=fx||{};const num=(v,d)=>(v===null||v===undefined||!Number.isFinite(Number(v)))?d:Number(v);
    return {k:clamp(num(fx.intensity,135),0,250)/135,size:clamp(num(fx.scale,100),25,250)/100,ox:clamp(num(fx.x,0),-100,100)/100*3,oy:clamp(num(fx.y,0),-100,100)/100*3,speed:clamp(num(fx.speed,100),0,300)/100};
  }
  /* v416: remember each character's last head frame so an effect clip keeps its place (and keeps drawing) when the
     character is deleted or not on stage at that moment. Without any known frame a default spot is used. */
  const evFrameCache={};
  window.__ethvelleCharFrame=function(ch){
    if(ch&&ch.__evGhost){
      const c=evFrameCache[String(ch.id)];
      if(c)return Object.assign({},c,{hand:Object.assign({},c.hand)});
      const W=Number(stageW)||360,H=Number(stageH)||640,r=Math.max(10,Math.min(W,H)*.1),cy=H*.32;
      return {x:W/2,y:cy,r:r,rot:0,flip:false,hand:{x:W/2+r*1.5,y:cy+r*1.6},face:faceGeom(null)};
    }
    const f=charFrame(ch);
    if(f&&ch&&ch.id!=null)evFrameCache[String(ch.id)]=f;
    return f;
  };
  window.__ethvelleCharGhost=function(id){return {__evGhost:true,id:id};};
  window.__ethvelleCharFxCfg=charFxCfg;
  function roundedGlow(ctx,x,y,r,color,alpha){const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(x-r*.2,y-r*.2,1,x,y,r);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,255,255,'+(alpha*.9)+')');g.addColorStop(.35,color.replace('ALPHA',String(alpha*.78)));g.addColorStop(1,color.replace('ALPHA','0'));ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();}
  function drawCharacterFx(ctx,char,t,w,h){
    /* Legacy per-character effect (projects saved before effects became timeline clips). */
    const fx=char&&char.visualEffects;if(!fx||!fx.type||fx.type==='none')return;
    const lib=window.ethvelleCharFxLib;if(!lib||!lib.has(fx.type))return;
    const frame=charFrame(char);if(!frame)return;
    const cfg=charFxCfg(fx);
    lib.draw(ctx,frame,fx.type,(Number(t)||0)*cfg.speed,cfg);
  }
  function drawSceneFx(ctx,type,t,w,h,settings){
    const cfg=settings&&typeof settings==='object'?settings:{intensity:settings};
    const intensity=clamp(cfg.intensity==null?135:cfg.intensity,0,250);
    if(intensity<=0||!type||type==='none'||type==='blur')return;
    const density=clamp(cfg.density==null?135:cfg.density,10,300)/100;
    const speed=clamp(cfg.speed==null?100:cfg.speed,0,300)/100;
    const scale=clamp(cfg.scale==null?100:cfg.scale,25,250)/100;
    const ox=clamp(cfg.x,-100,100)/100*w*.34,oy=clamp(cfg.y,-100,100)/100*h*.34;
    const tm=(Number(t)||0)*speed;
    // Older projects used 0 - 100 intensity. Treat their previous 70% default as a full,
    // lively baseline while allowing the expanded 0 - 250 slider to go substantially stronger.
    const strength=Math.min(3,intensity/70);
    const hash=i=>{const x=Math.sin((i+1)*127.1+37.7)*43758.5453;return x-Math.floor(x);};
    const wrap=(v,m)=>((v%m)+m)%m;
    const rgba=(r,g,b,a)=>`rgba(${r},${g},${b},${Math.max(0,Math.min(1,a))})`;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(w*.5+ox,h*.5+oy);ctx.scale(scale,scale);ctx.translate(-w*.5,-h*.5);
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';

    function cloudBand(dark,layer){
      const baseY=h*(.12+layer*.13),travel=(dark?14:9)*(layer+1)*tm,alpha=(dark?.13:.075)*strength/(layer+1);
      const grad=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,h*.74);
      if(dark){grad.addColorStop(0,rgba(9,17,31,alpha*1.4));grad.addColorStop(.42,rgba(31,43,62,alpha));grad.addColorStop(1,'rgba(24,34,48,0)');}
      else{grad.addColorStop(0,rgba(224,235,249,alpha));grad.addColorStop(.7,'rgba(210,226,242,0)');}
      ctx.fillStyle=grad;ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h*.76);
      const count=Math.min(20,Math.max(4,Math.round((dark?8:10)*density/1.35))),puffW=w*(.11+.04*layer),puffH=h*(.055+.013*layer),span=w+puffW*3;
      for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
        const x=wrap(i*span/count+travel,span)-puffW*1.2;
        const y=baseY+((hash(i+layer*20)-.5)*h*.075)+Math.sin(tm*.12+i*.8)*h*.012;
        const shade=dark?rgba(28+layer*5,38+layer*5,55+layer*8,alpha*1.3):rgba(226,235,248,alpha*1.6);
        // Layered overlapping puffs stay soft without a per-puff canvas blur filter, which is
        // expensive on mobile GPU compositors.
        ctx.save();ctx.fillStyle=shade;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.ellipse(x,y,puffW*.72,puffH*.50,0,0,Math.PI*2);
        ctx.ellipse(x-puffW*.33,y-puffH*.26,puffW*.40,puffH*.62,0,0,Math.PI*2);
        ctx.ellipse(x+puffW*.05,y-puffH*.53,puffW*.45,puffH*.75,0,0,Math.PI*2);
        ctx.ellipse(x+puffW*.42,y-puffH*.20,puffW*.37,puffH*.58,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
        if(dark){ctx.strokeStyle=rgba(129,154,188,alpha*.38);ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,h*.0018);ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,y+puffH*.08,puffW*.64,puffH*.25,0,0,Math.PI);ctx.stroke();}
      }
    }
    function rainLayer(count,layer,storm){
      const fall=(storm?520:365)*(0.42+layer*.46)*speed;
      const drift=(storm?155:64)*(0.35+layer*.5)*speed;
      const lineLen=(storm?17:11)*(0.6+layer*.58)*scale;
      const alpha=(storm?.25:.17)*strength*(.35+layer*.36);
      ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(.65,(w/1100)*(layer+1)*.8);
      for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
        const seed=hash(i+layer*701),x=wrap(seed*(w+140)+tm*drift,w+140)-70;
        const y=wrap(hash(i+layer*1701)*(h+100)+tm*fall,h+100)-50;
        ctx.strokeStyle=rgba(layer===2?218:174,layer===2?232:210,255,alpha*(.65+seed*.55));
        ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y);ctx.lineTo(x-drift/fall*lineLen,y+lineLen);ctx.stroke();
        if(layer===2&&i%7===0){ctx.fillStyle=rgba(190,220,255,alpha*.55);ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x-drift/fall*lineLen,y+lineLen,2.3,1.1,-.25,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();}
      }
    }
    function lightning(){
      const cycle=4.4,phase=wrap(tm,cycle),pulse=phase<.085?1:phase>.13&&phase<.19?.48:phase>.25&&phase<.285?.22:0;
      if(!pulse)return;
      const flashAlpha=Math.min(.36,.13*strength*pulse);
      ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='screen';ctx.fillStyle=rgba(206,226,255,flashAlpha);ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
      const rootX=w*(.59+hash(Math.floor(tm/cycle))*0.25),rootY=0;
      ctx.shadowColor='#9FCBFF';ctx.shadowBlur=Math.max(12,w*.035);ctx.strokeStyle=rgba(218,239,255,.92*pulse);ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,w*.0028);ctx.lineJoin='round';
      const pts=[[rootX,rootY]];let x=rootX,y=0;
      for(let i=0;i<9;i++){x+=(hash(i+Math.floor(tm/cycle)*13)-.5)*w*.095;y+=h*.105;pts.push([x,y]);}
      ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(...pts[0]);for(let i=1;i<pts.length;i++)ctx.lineTo(...pts[i]);ctx.stroke();
      // Short side forks give the strike a branching, irregular silhouette.
      ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.3,w*.0016);ctx.shadowBlur=Math.max(5,w*.018);
      [2,4,6].forEach((at,j)=>{const p=pts[at],dir=j%2?-1:1;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p[0],p[1]);ctx.lineTo(p[0]+dir*w*(.06+j*.012),p[1]+h*(.06+j*.016));ctx.lineTo(p[0]+dir*w*(.075+j*.014),p[1]+h*(.115+j*.018));ctx.stroke();});
      const glow=ctx.createRadialGradient(rootX,h*.35,2,rootX,h*.35,Math.max(w,h)*.62);glow.addColorStop(0,rgba(194,220,255,.20*pulse*strength));glow.addColorStop(1,'rgba(175,210,255,0)');ctx.fillStyle=glow;ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);ctx.restore();
    }

    if(window.__evSceneFxPack&&window.__evSceneFxPack.has(type)){window.__evSceneFxPack.draw(ctx,type,tm,w,h,{strength:strength,density:density,scale:1,speed:speed});ctx.restore();return;}
    if(type==='clouds'||type==='sunrise'){cloudBand(false,0);cloudBand(false,1);if(type==='clouds')cloudBand(false,2);}
    if(type==='storm'){cloudBand(true,0);cloudBand(true,1);cloudBand(true,2);}
    if(type==='rain'){
      const wash=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,h);wash.addColorStop(0,rgba(38,85,136,.06*strength));wash.addColorStop(1,rgba(8,26,49,.13*strength));ctx.fillStyle=wash;ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
      rainLayer(Math.min(76,Math.round(48*density)),0,false);rainLayer(Math.min(90,Math.round(55*density)),1,false);rainLayer(Math.min(65,Math.round(40*density)),2,false);
    }
    if(type==='storm'){
      const shade=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,h);shade.addColorStop(0,rgba(4,10,23,.30*strength));shade.addColorStop(.55,rgba(12,25,45,.19*strength));shade.addColorStop(1,rgba(7,15,28,.24*strength));ctx.fillStyle=shade;ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
      rainLayer(Math.min(80,Math.round(54*density)),0,true);rainLayer(Math.min(100,Math.round(68*density)),1,true);rainLayer(Math.min(70,Math.round(44*density)),2,true);lightning();
    }
    if(type==='lightning')lightning();
    if(type==='snow'){
      const count=Math.min(220,Math.round(95*density));
      for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
        const z=.25+hash(i+77)*1.15,r=(.7+hash(i+211)*2.2)*z*scale,x=wrap(hash(i+331)*(w+50)+Math.sin(tm*.45+i)*10*speed,w+50)-25,y=wrap(hash(i+449)*(h+50)+tm*(12+z*35),h+50)-25;
        ctx.fillStyle=rgba(236,246,255,(.28+.48*z/1.4)*strength);ctx.shadowColor='#C8E7FF';ctx.shadowBlur=r*2;
        ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
        if(z>1.05&&i%5===0){ctx.strokeStyle=rgba(255,255,255,.55*strength);ctx.lineWidth=.7;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-r*2,y);ctx.lineTo(x+r*2,y);ctx.moveTo(x,y-r*2);ctx.lineTo(x,y+r*2);ctx.stroke();}
      }
      ctx.shadowBlur=0;
    }
    if(type==='flash'){
      const phase=wrap(tm,3.25),pulse=phase<.06?1:phase>.13&&phase<.19?.62:phase>.24&&phase<.275?.28:0;
      if(pulse){ctx.globalCompositeOperation='screen';const bloom=ctx.createRadialGradient(w*.53,h*.37,2,w*.53,h*.37,Math.max(w,h)*.82);bloom.addColorStop(0,rgba(255,255,244,.62*pulse*strength));bloom.addColorStop(.34,rgba(255,225,188,.24*pulse*strength));bloom.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,220,174,0)');ctx.fillStyle=bloom;ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);}
    }
    if(type==='fog'){
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation='screen';
      const fogLayers=Math.min(7,Math.max(3,Math.round(5*density/1.35)));
      for(let layer=0;layer<fogLayers;layer++){
        const y=h*(.42+layer*.125),count=Math.min(6,Math.max(3,Math.round(4*density/1.35))),x0=wrap(tm*(8+layer*4)+layer*w*.28,w*1.55)-w*.28;
        for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
          const x=x0+i*w*.42+Math.sin(tm*.12+layer+i)*w*.025,yy=y+Math.sin(tm*.18+i*1.4+layer)*h*.018,rx=w*(.27+layer*.018),ry=h*(.07+layer*.008);
          const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(x,yy,1,x,yy,rx);g.addColorStop(0,rgba(210,229,245,.13*strength));g.addColorStop(.55,rgba(194,219,239,.065*strength));g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(190,215,235,0)');ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(x-rx,yy-ry*2,rx*2,ry*4);
        }
      }
    }
    if(type==='sunrise'){
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation='screen';
      const sky=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,h);sky.addColorStop(0,rgba(89,118,194,.09*strength));sky.addColorStop(.36,rgba(241,111,100,.22*strength));sky.addColorStop(.68,rgba(255,170,92,.23*strength));sky.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,213,144,0)');ctx.fillStyle=sky;ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
      const sx=w*.73,sy=h*.34,sr=Math.min(w,h)*.16,pulse=.97+.03*Math.sin(tm*.6);const sun=ctx.createRadialGradient(sx,sy,2,sx,sy,sr*2.7);sun.addColorStop(0,rgba(255,250,214,.62*strength));sun.addColorStop(.23,rgba(255,207,132,.37*strength));sun.addColorStop(.6,rgba(255,130,105,.12*strength));sun.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,128,100,0)');ctx.fillStyle=sun;ctx.fillRect(sx-sr*3,sy-sr*3,sr*6,sr*6);
      ctx.fillStyle=rgba(255,239,207,.24*strength);ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(sx,sy,sr*.48*pulse,sr*.48*pulse,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  function drawOverlaySceneFx(targetCtx,ov,t,ow,oh){
    if(!targetCtx||!ov||!ov.visualEffects||!ov.visualEffects.type||ov.visualEffects.type==='none'||ov.visualEffects.type==='blur')return;
    if(ov.ethvelleEffectClip&&ov.ethvelleEffectKind==='character'){if(typeof window.ethvelleDrawCharacterEffectClip==='function')window.ethvelleDrawCharacterEffectClip(targetCtx,ov,t);return;}
    /* v416: media effects are independent clips - they keep drawing for their own duration even if the clip they were created on is moved, trimmed or deleted. */
    const sx=ow/Math.max(1,stageW),sy=oh/Math.max(1,stageH);
    let keyed=null,tracked=null,tr=null;
    try{keyed=typeof window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue==='function'?window.ethvelleGetObjectKeyframeValue(ov,'transform',t):null;}catch(_e){}
    try{if(ov.clipTracking&&ov.clipTracking.enabled&&ov.clipTracking.targetId&&typeof window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform==='function')tracked=window.ethvelleGetTrackedOverlayTransform(ov,t,keyed);}catch(_e){}
    tr=(tracked||keyed||ov.transform||{});
    let motion={x:0,y:0,rotation:0,scale:1};
    try{if(typeof window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState==='function')motion=window.getEthvelleClipAnimationState(ov,t)||motion;}catch(_e){}
    const ox=(Number(tr.offsetX)||0)+(Number(motion.x)||0),oy=(Number(tr.offsetY)||0)+(Number(motion.y)||0);
    const scale=Math.max(.0001,(Number(tr.scale)||1)*(Number(motion.scale)||1));
    const rotation=(Number(tr.rotation)||0)+(Number(motion.rotation)||0);
    let iw=Number(ov.sourceWidth)||0,ih=Number(ov.sourceHeight)||0;
    try{
      const media=(typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'&&overlayMediaEls)?overlayMediaEls[String(ov.id)]:null;
      if(media){iw=iw||Number(media.videoWidth||media.naturalWidth||media.width)||0;ih=ih||Number(media.videoHeight||media.naturalHeight||media.height)||0;}
    }catch(_e){}
    let cw=ow,ch=oh;
    if(iw>0&&ih>0){const fit=Math.min(ow/iw,oh/ih);cw=iw*fit;ch=ih*fit;}
    const cx=(ow-cw)/2,cy=(oh-ch)/2;
    let crop={left:0,right:0,top:0,bottom:0};
    try{crop=window.ethvelleCropFractions?window.ethvelleCropFractions(ov.crop):(ov.crop||crop)||crop;}catch(_e){}
    const left=Math.max(0,Math.min(.92,Number(crop.left)||0)),right=Math.max(0,Math.min(.92,Number(crop.right)||0));
    const top=Math.max(0,Math.min(.92,Number(crop.top)||0)),bottom=Math.max(0,Math.min(.92,Number(crop.bottom)||0));
    const visibleX=cx+left*cw,visibleY=cy+top*ch,visibleW=Math.max(1,cw*(1-left-right)),visibleH=Math.max(1,ch*(1-top-bottom));
    targetCtx.save();
    targetCtx.translate(ow/2+ox*sx,oh/2+oy*sy);
    if(rotation)targetCtx.rotate(rotation*Math.PI/180);
    targetCtx.scale(scale*(ov.mirrorX?-1:1),scale*(ov.mirrorY?-1:1));
    targetCtx.translate(-ow/2,-oh/2);
    // Keep particles/light/fog inside the actual cropped media rectangle, not the whole stage.
    targetCtx.beginPath();targetCtx.rect(visibleX,visibleY,visibleW,visibleH);targetCtx.clip();
    if(ov.visualMask&&typeof window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip==='function')window.ethvelleApplyCanvasMaskClip(targetCtx,ov.visualMask,ow,oh);
    targetCtx.translate(visibleX,visibleY);
    drawSceneFx(targetCtx,ov.visualEffects.type,t,visibleW,visibleH,ov.visualEffects);
    targetCtx.restore();
  }
  /* v413: shadowBlur and ctx.filter blur are NOT scaled by ctx.scale(), so at 1080p glows would look thin.
     While effects are drawn, scale them by the same factor as the drawing. */
  function evFxScaleBlur(ctx,k){
    try{
      ctx.__evFxK=k;
      if(ctx.__evFxPatched)return;
      const P=Object.getPrototypeOf(ctx),sb=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(P,'shadowBlur'),fl=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(P,'filter');
      if(sb&&sb.get&&sb.set)Object.defineProperty(ctx,'shadowBlur',{configurable:true,get(){return sb.get.call(ctx)/(ctx.__evFxK||1);},set(v){sb.set.call(ctx,(Number(v)||0)*(ctx.__evFxK||1));}});
      if(fl&&fl.get&&fl.set)Object.defineProperty(ctx,'filter',{configurable:true,get(){return fl.get.call(ctx);},set(v){const q=ctx.__evFxK||1;fl.set.call(ctx,(q!==1&&typeof v==='string')?v.replace(/(-?\d*\.?\d+)px/g,(m,n)=>(parseFloat(n)*q)+'px'):v);}});
      ctx.__evFxPatched=true;
    }catch(_e){}
  }
  window.ethvelleDrawVisualEffectsToContext=function(targetCtx,tSec,outW,outH){
    if(!targetCtx||!stageW||!stageH)return;const t=Number(tSec)||0,ow=outW||stageW,oh=outH||stageH;
    evFxScaleBlur(targetCtx,ow/stageW);
    const timed=!!(targetCtx.canvas&&targetCtx.canvas.id==='evcaV70FxCanvas'),t0=timed?performance.now():0;
    try{return drawVisualEffectsInner(targetCtx,t,ow,oh);}finally{if(targetCtx.__evFxK)targetCtx.__evFxK=1;if(timed)evFxAdapt(performance.now()-t0);}
  };
  function drawVisualEffectsInner(targetCtx,t,ow,oh){
    targetCtx.save();targetCtx.scale(ow/stageW,oh/stageH);
    if(Array.isArray(backgrounds)&&typeof currentBackgroundClip==='function'){
      const bg=currentBackgroundClip(t);if(bg&&bg.visualEffects&&bg.visualEffects.type&&bg.visualEffects.type!=='none')drawSceneFx(targetCtx,bg.visualEffects.type,t,stageW,stageH,bg.visualEffects);
    }
    if(Array.isArray(overlays))overlays.forEach(o=>{if(o&&['image','video','text','shape','color'].includes(o.type)&&t>=Number(o.trimStart||0)&&t<Number(o.trimEnd||0)&&o.visualEffects&&o.visualEffects.type&&o.visualEffects.type!=='none')drawOverlaySceneFx(targetCtx,o,t,stageW,stageH);});
    if(Array.isArray(characters))characters.forEach(c=>drawCharacterFx(targetCtx,c,t,stageW,stageH));
    targetCtx.restore();
  };
  /* v413: effects preview is drawn at 1080p or higher (short side >= 1080px, long side capped at 2160px)
     instead of the small stage size, then shown scaled down to the stage. */
  window.__evFxQ=.5;
  window.ethvelleFxPreviewSize=function(sw,sh){
    sw=Math.max(1,Number(sw)||1);sh=Math.max(1,Number(sh)||1);
    /* v426: timeline scrubbing uses a fixed preview tier. Do not follow isPlaying,
       adaptive quality or per-effect Quality values here; changing canvas dimensions
       on every scrub frame forces a costly canvas reallocation on mobile. */
    if(window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing){
      const scrubScale=1.7;
      return [Math.max(2,Math.round(sw*scrubScale)),Math.max(2,Math.round(sh*scrubScale))];
    }
    let kHi=Math.max(1,1080/Math.min(sw,sh));
    if(Math.max(sw,sh)*kHi>2160)kHi=Math.max(1,2160/Math.max(sw,sh));
    /* v414: paused / still frames are drawn at full 1080p. While playing, the resolution adapts to how fast the
       device can draw the effects, so playback never lags. */
    let playing=false;try{playing=!!isPlaying;}catch(_e){}
    const q=playing?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,window.__evFxQ)):1;
    /* v415: Character Effects "Quality" slider (25-150%, default 100%) scales the effect-canvas resolution. */
    const k=(1+(kHi-1)*q)*(window.__evCharFxQuality?window.__evCharFxQuality():1);
    let w=sw*k,h=sh*k;const cap=3240/Math.max(w,h);if(cap<1){w*=cap;h*=cap;}
    return [Math.max(2,Math.round(w)),Math.max(2,Math.round(h))];
  };
  window.__evCharFxQuality=function(){
    let best=0,found=false;
    try{
      const now=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
      (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).forEach(o=>{
        if(!o||!o.ethvelleEffectClip||o.ethvelleEffectKind!=='character')return;
        const fx=o.visualEffects;if(!fx||!fx.type||fx.type==='none')return;
        if(now<Number(o.trimStart||0)||now>=Number(o.trimEnd||0))return;
        const v=Number(fx.quality);found=true;best=Math.max(best,Number.isFinite(v)&&v>0?v:100);
      });
    }catch(_e){}
    return found?Math.max(25,Math.min(150,best))/100:1;
  };
  /* v414: drawStick(), renderFrame() and the drag preview each repainted the effect canvas, so one frame could draw
     every effect 2-3 times. Only the first paint of a frame runs; a skipped one is replayed once shortly after. */
  window.__evFxGate=function(fn){
    const pn=performance.now();let now=0;
    try{now=Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;}catch(_e){}
    const g=window.__evFxG||(window.__evFxG={t:NaN,p:0,timer:0});
    if(g.t===now&&pn-g.p<40){
