<script id="ethvelle-v72-deep-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const n=(v,f=0)=>{const x=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(x)?x:f;};

  /* ---------- Gesture-safe video preview ---------- */
  function attemptVideoPlay(video,allowMutedFallback=true){
    if(!video||video.tagName!=='VIDEO')return Promise.resolve(false);
    const ov=(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(item=>item&&String(item.id)===String(video.dataset.evV72OverlayId||''));
    if(typeof window.evSafePlayOverlayVideo==='function')return window.evSafePlayOverlayVideo(video,ov);
    return Promise.resolve(false);
  }
  window.ethvelleV72PlayVideo=attemptVideoPlay;

  function primeActiveVideosFromGesture(){
    if(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)return;
    let now=n(typeof currentAbsoluteTimeSec!=='undefined'?currentAbsoluteTimeSec:0);
    // If a video clip is selected but the playhead is outside that clip, begin at its start.
    // This prevents a selected video later on the timeline from appearing frozen/blank when
    // the user presses Play without first scrubbing into the clip.
    const selectedVideo=(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).find(o=>o&&o.type==='video'&&String(o.id)===String(typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'?selectedClip:''));
    if(selectedVideo&&(now<n(selectedVideo.trimStart)||now>=n(selectedVideo.trimEnd))){
      try{setCurrentAbsoluteTime(n(selectedVideo.trimStart));now=n(selectedVideo.trimStart);}catch(_e){}
    }
    try{if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}catch(err){console.warn('Ethvelle video pre-render failed',err);}
    // Ensure the video overlay layer has synchronously created the active <video> elements
    // before calling play() in the trusted button gesture.
    try{if(typeof renderOverlayMedia==='function')renderOverlayMedia(now);}catch(_e){}
    const active=(Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).filter(o=>o&&o.type==='video'&&now>=n(o.trimStart)&&now<n(o.trimEnd));
    active.forEach(ov=>{
      let el=null;try{el=typeof overlayMediaEls!=='undefined'?overlayMediaEls[ov.id]:null;}catch(_e){}
      if(!el||el.tagName!=='VIDEO')return;
      const speed=n(ov.speed,1)||1;
      const max=Math.max(.05,n(ov.sourceDuration,el.duration||n(ov.trimEnd)-n(ov.trimStart))-.02);
      const local=Math.max(0,Math.min(max,n(ov.sourceOffset)+(now-n(ov.trimStart))*speed));
      try{if(el.readyState>=1&&Number.isFinite(local)&&Math.abs(el.currentTime-local)>.04){el.muted=true;el.pause();el.currentTime=local;}}catch(_e){}
      el.playsInline=true;el.autoplay=false;el.setAttribute('playsinline','');el.setAttribute('webkit-playsinline','');el.removeAttribute('autoplay');el.preload='auto';
      const retryWhenReady=()=>{if(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)attemptVideoPlay(el,true);};
      el.addEventListener('loadedmetadata',retryWhenReady,{once:true});
      el.addEventListener('loadeddata',retryWhenReady,{once:true});
      el.addEventListener('canplay',retryWhenReady,{once:true});
      // The trusted gesture may pre-roll muted; evSafePlayVideo will not unmute until
      // playback is active, the cut gate is clear and source position is within 40ms.
      attemptVideoPlay(el,true);
    });
  }
  window.ethvelleV72PrimeVideoPlayback=primeActiveVideosFromGesture;
  const originalTogglePlayback=window.togglePlayback;
  if(typeof originalTogglePlayback==='function'&&!originalTogglePlayback.__evv73VideoGesture){
    const gestureToggle=function(){if(!isPlaying)primeActiveVideosFromGesture();return originalTogglePlayback.apply(this,arguments);};
    gestureToggle.__evv73VideoGesture=true;window.togglePlayback=gestureToggle;
  }
  const playButton=$('playBtn');
  if(playButton&&!playButton.dataset.evV72PrimeBound){
    playButton.dataset.evV72PrimeBound='1';
    playButton.addEventListener('pointerdown',primeActiveVideosFromGesture,{capture:true,passive:true});
    playButton.addEventListener('click',primeActiveVideosFromGesture,{capture:true});
  }
  // Retry when a newly created media element finishes decoding after playback has started.
  const mediaLayer=$('overlayMediaLayer');
  if(mediaLayer&&!mediaLayer.dataset.evV72CanplayObserver){
    mediaLayer.dataset.evV72CanplayObserver='1';
    const retryActiveVideo=e=>{
      const v=e.target;
      if(v&&v.tagName==='VIDEO'&&v.dataset.evWarmElement!=='1'&&typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)attemptVideoPlay(v,true);
    };
    mediaLayer.addEventListener('loadeddata',retryActiveVideo,true);
    mediaLayer.addEventListener('canplay',retryActiveVideo,true);
  }

  /* ---------- Frame capture directly in the bottom options row ---------- */
  function makeFrameTool(id,label,title,icon,handler){
    const item=document.createElement('div');item.id=id;item.className='tool-item evv72-frame-tool';
    item.setAttribute('role','button');item.tabIndex=0;item.title=title;item.setAttribute('aria-label',title);
    item.innerHTML=icon+'<span>'+label+'</span>';
    const run=e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();try{handler();}catch(err){console.error('Frame toolbar action failed',err);}};
    item.addEventListener('click',run);item.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){run(e);}});
    return item;
  }
  const downloadIcon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 3v12m-5-5 5 5 5-5M4 20h16"/></svg>';
  const layerIcon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="3.5" y="3.5" width="17" height="17" rx="2"/><path d="M12 7.5v9M7.5 12h9"/></svg>';
  function ensureFrameToolbarButtons(){
    [['bottomToolsDefault','default'],['bottomToolsClip','clip'],['bottomToolsKeyframe','keyframe']].forEach(([hostId,suffix])=>{
      const row=$(hostId);if(!row)return;
      const saveId='evv72SaveFrame-'+suffix,layerId='evv72AddFrame-'+suffix;
      const anchor=suffix==='default'?row.querySelector('[data-tool=\"model\"]'):suffix==='clip'?$('clipSplitTool'):row.children[1];
      let save=$(saveId),layer=$(layerId);
      if(!save)save=makeFrameTool(saveId,'Save PNG','Save current canvas frame as a PNG file',downloadIcon,()=>window.ethvelleSaveCanvasFrame&&window.ethvelleSaveCanvasFrame());
      if(!layer)layer=makeFrameTool(layerId,'Add Layer','Add the current canvas frame as a new image layer',layerIcon,()=>window.ethvelleAddCanvasFrameAsLayer&&window.ethvelleAddCanvasFrameAsLayer());
      if(anchor&&anchor.parentNode===row){row.insertBefore(save,anchor);row.insertBefore(layer,anchor);}
      else{if(save.parentNode!==row)row.appendChild(save);if(layer.parentNode!==row)row.appendChild(layer);}
    });
  }
  const priorSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof priorSync==='function'&&!priorSync.__evv72FrameTools){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=priorSync.apply(this,arguments);ensureFrameToolbarButtons();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv72FrameTools=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',ensureFrameToolbarButtons,{once:true});
  else ensureFrameToolbarButtons();

})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v76-recording-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const q=(root,sel)=>root?root.querySelector(sel):null;
  const qa=(root,sel)=>root?[...root.querySelectorAll(sel)]:[];
  const screen=$('screen-editor');

  /* ---------- Single Frame Capture sheet ---------- */
  function ensureFrameSheet(){
    if($('evcaFrameCaptureSheet'))return $('evcaFrameCaptureSheet');
    if(!screen)return null;
    const sheet=document.createElement('div');
    sheet.id='evcaFrameCaptureSheet';
    sheet.innerHTML=`
      <div class="evca-frame-sheet" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Frame Capture">
        <div class="evca-frame-sheet-head"><span>Frame Capture</span><button type="button" class="evca-frame-sheet-close" aria-label="Close"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6 6 18"/></svg></button></div>
        <button type="button" class="evca-frame-action" data-frame-action="layer">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="3.5" y="3.5" width="17" height="17" rx="2"/><path d="M12 7.5v9M7.5 12h9"/></svg>
          <span class="evca-fa-text"><strong>Add as Layer</strong></span>
        </button>
        <button type="button" class="evca-frame-action" data-frame-action="background">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="16" rx="2"/><path d="M3 15l5-5 4 4 3-3 6 6"/><circle cx="8.5" cy="8.5" r="1.3"/></svg>
          <span class="evca-fa-text"><strong>Add as BG</strong></span>
        </button>
        <button type="button" class="evca-frame-action" data-frame-action="save">
          <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><path d="M12 3v12m-5-5 5 5 5-5M4 20h16"/></svg>
          <span class="evca-fa-text"><strong>Save Frame</strong></span>
        </button>
      </div>`;
    sheet.addEventListener('click',e=>{
      if(e.target===sheet||e.target.closest('.evca-frame-sheet-close')){closeFrameSheet();return;}
      const action=e.target.closest('[data-frame-action]')?.dataset.frameAction;
      if(!action)return;
      closeFrameSheet();
      try{
        if(action==='layer'&&typeof window.ethvelleAddCanvasFrameAsLayer==='function')window.ethvelleAddCanvasFrameAsLayer();
        else if(action==='background'&&typeof window.ethvelleAddCanvasFrameAsBackground==='function')window.ethvelleAddCanvasFrameAsBackground();
        else if(action==='save'&&typeof window.ethvelleSaveCanvasFrame==='function')window.ethvelleSaveCanvasFrame();
        else if(typeof window.showToast==='function')window.showToast('Frame capture is not ready yet',{duration:1600});
      }catch(err){console.error('Frame capture action failed',err);}
    });
    screen.appendChild(sheet);
    return sheet;
  }
  function openFrameSheet(){const sheet=ensureFrameSheet();if(sheet){sheet.classList.add('active');sheet.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');}}
  function closeFrameSheet(){const sheet=$('evcaFrameCaptureSheet');if(sheet){sheet.classList.remove('active');sheet.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');}}
  window.openEthvelleFrameCapture=openFrameSheet;
  window.closeEthvelleFrameCapture=closeFrameSheet;

  const frameIcon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"><rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="2"/><path d="M8 8h8v8H8zM12 2v3m0 14v3M2 12h3m14 0h3"/></svg>';
  function replaceFrameToolbarItems(){
    [['bottomToolsDefault','default'],['bottomToolsClip','clip'],['bottomToolsKeyframe','keyframe']].forEach(([hostId,kind])=>{
      const host=$(hostId);if(!host)return;
      // Remove a legacy button once, then reuse the replacement on every later toolbar sync.
      qa(host,'.evv72-frame-tool').forEach(el=>el.remove());
      let btn=$('evcaFrameCaptureTool-'+kind);
      if(!btn){
        btn=document.createElement('div');
        btn.className='tool-item evca-frame-capture-tool';
        btn.id='evcaFrameCaptureTool-'+kind;
        btn.setAttribute('role','button');btn.tabIndex=0;
        btn.title='Capture the current canvas frame';
        btn.setAttribute('aria-label','Frame Capture');
        btn.innerHTML=frameIcon+'<span>Frame</span>';
        btn.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();openFrameSheet();});
        btn.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();openFrameSheet();}});
      }
      // Keep exactly one node per row; do not tear down the row during frequent selection syncs.
      qa(host,'.evca-frame-capture-tool').forEach(el=>{if(el!==btn)el.remove();});
      const anchor=kind==='default'?q(host,'[data-tool="media"]'):kind==='clip'?$ ('clipMediaAccessTool'):$('keyframeMediaAccessTool');
      if(anchor&&anchor.parentNode===host){if(btn.previousElementSibling!==anchor)anchor.insertAdjacentElement('afterend',btn);}
      else if(btn.parentNode!==host)host.appendChild(btn);
    });
  }
  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__evv75FrameCapture){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);replaceFrameToolbarItems();return result;};
    wrapped.__evv75FrameCapture=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  replaceFrameToolbarItems();

  /* Keep the single transport-row zoom button visible regardless of selected layer type. */
  function syncCanvasZoomEntry(){
    const wrap=q(screen,'.canvas-wrap'),button=$('transportZoomBtn');
    if(!button)return;
    const active=!!(wrap?.classList.contains('evca-inline-zoom')||screen?.classList.contains('evca-inline-zoom-active'));
    button.style.display='inline-flex';
    button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(active));
    button.title=active?'Exit canvas zoom':'Zoom canvas';
  }
  const oldToggle=window.toggleCanvasZoomView;
  if(typeof oldToggle==='function'&&!oldToggle.__evv75Zoom){
    const wrapped=function(){const result=oldToggle.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>{syncCanvasZoomEntry();ensureSingleZoomFrameButton();});return result;};
    wrapped.__evv75Zoom=true;window.toggleCanvasZoomView=wrapped;
  }
  function ensureSingleZoomFrameButton(){
    const hud=$('evcaInlineZoomHud');if(!hud)return;
    // v71 used to add two separate Save and Layer buttons. Replace them with one shared entry.
    ['evcaZoomSave','evcaZoomLayer'].forEach(id=>$(id)?.remove());
    let button=$('evcaZoomFrameCapture');
    if(!button){
      button=document.createElement('button');button.type='button';button.id='evcaZoomFrameCapture';
      button.className='evca-zoom-frame-open';button.title='Frame Capture';button.setAttribute('aria-label','Frame Capture');
      button.innerHTML=frameIcon;
      button.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();openFrameSheet();});
    }
    const controlBar=hud.querySelector('.evca-zoom-control-bar');
    if(controlBar&&button.parentNode!==controlBar)controlBar.appendChild(button);
  }
  const oldOpen=window.openCustomAnimationStudio,oldClose=window.closeCustomAnimationStudio;
  if(typeof oldOpen==='function'&&!oldOpen.__evv75ZoomSync){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldOpen.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>{syncCanvasZoomEntry();ensureSingleZoomFrameButton();});return r;};
    wrapped.__evv75ZoomSync=true;window.openCustomAnimationStudio=wrapped;
  }
  if(typeof oldClose==='function'&&!oldClose.__evv75ZoomSync){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldClose.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(syncCanvasZoomEntry);return r;};
    wrapped.__evv75ZoomSync=true;window.closeCustomAnimationStudio=wrapped;
  }
  syncCanvasZoomEntry();

  /* ---------- Selection follows the clip that passes under the fixed playhead ---------- */
  function currentSelectedRef(){
    try{return typeof selectedClip!=='undefined'&&selectedClip!=null?String(selectedClip):'';}catch(_e){return '';}
  }
  function selectedElementForRef(ref){
    const root=$('timelineInner');if(!root||!ref)return null;
    // A selected clip of a NON-active character (readonly strip) must resolve to ITS OWN row,
    // otherwise the active character (the fox) steals the selection while sliding.
    const ownerRaw=window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionCharacterIndex;
    if(ownerRaw!=null&&Number.isInteger(Number(ownerRaw))&&Number(ownerRaw)!==Number(activeCharIdx)&&(ref==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(ref))){
      const own=root.querySelector('.char-row[data-char-idx="'+Number(ownerRaw)+'"] .anim-clip-readonly[data-readonly-clip-ref="'+ref+'"]');
      if(own)return own;
    }
    if(ref==='main')return $('animClipEl');
    if(ref.startsWith('seg'))return root.querySelector('.anim-clip[data-seg-idx="'+ref.slice(3)+'"]');
    const overlay=qa(root,'.overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]').find(el=>String(el.dataset.overlayId)===ref);
    if(overlay)return overlay;
    const bg=qa(root,'.track-clip[data-bg-id]').find(el=>String(el.dataset.bgId)===ref);
    if(bg)return bg;
    const char=ref.match(/^char(\d+)$/);
    if(char)return root.querySelector('.char-row[data-char-idx="'+char[1]+'"]');
    return null;
  }
  function refForClip(el){
    if(!el)return null;
    if(el.id==='animClipEl')return 'main';
    if(el.classList.contains('anim-clip-readonly')&&el.dataset.readonlyClipRef!=null)return String(el.dataset.readonlyClipRef);
    if(el.classList.contains('anim-clip')&&el.dataset.segIdx!=null)return 'seg'+el.dataset.segIdx;
    if(el.classList.contains('overlay-clip')&&el.dataset.overlayId!=null)return String(el.dataset.overlayId);
    if(el.classList.contains('track-clip')&&el.dataset.bgId!=null)return String(el.dataset.bgId);
    return null;
  }
  function rowForPointer(x,y){
    const hit=document.elementFromPoint(x,y);
    return hit&&hit.closest?hit.closest('.overlay-track-row,#backgroundRow,.char-row'):null;
  }
  function timelineEntriesForRow(row){
    if(!row)return [];
    if(row.id==='backgroundRow'){
      return (Array.isArray(backgrounds)?backgrounds:[]).map(model=>({id:String(model.id),model,characterIndex:null,kind:'background'}));
    }
    if(row.classList.contains('overlay-track-row')){
      const group=String(row.dataset.group||'clips');
      return (Array.isArray(overlays)?overlays:[]).filter(model=>ethvelleOverlayGroupKey(model)===group)
        .map(model=>({id:String(model.id),model,characterIndex:null,kind:'overlay'}));
    }
    if(row.classList.contains('char-row')){
      const idx=Number(row.dataset.charIdx);
      if(!Number.isInteger(idx)||!Array.isArray(characters)||!characters[idx])return [];
      const model=characters[idx];
      const clip=idx===Number(activeCharIdx)?activeClip:model.clip;
      const segments=idx===Number(activeCharIdx)?splitStickmanSegments:model.segments;
      if(typeof getAllStickmanClips!=='function')return [];
      return getAllStickmanClips(clip,segments).map(entry=>({id:String(entry.ref),model:entry.clip,characterIndex:idx,kind:'character'}));
    }
    return [];
  }
  function timelineEntryElement(row,entry){
    if(!row||!entry)return null;
    if(entry.kind==='background')return qa(row,'.track-clip[data-bg-id]').find(el=>String(el.dataset.bgId)===entry.id)||null;
    if(entry.kind==='overlay')return qa(row,'.overlay-clip[data-overlay-id]').find(el=>String(el.dataset.overlayId)===entry.id)||null;
    if(entry.kind==='character'){
      return qa(row,'.anim-clip,.anim-clip-readonly').find(el=>{
        const ref=el.dataset.readonlyClipRef!=null?String(el.dataset.readonlyClipRef):(el.id==='animClipEl'?'main':el.dataset.segIdx!=null?'seg'+el.dataset.segIdx:'');
        return ref===entry.id;
      })||null;
    }
    return null;
  }
  function resolveTimelineClipAtTime(row,x,y){
    if(!row)return null;
    const now=typeof getCurrentAbsoluteTime==='function'?Number(getCurrentAbsoluteTime()):Number(currentAbsoluteTimeSec)||0;
    const entries=timelineEntriesForRow(row).filter(entry=>{
      const start=Number(entry.model&&entry.model.trimStart)||0;
      const end=Number(entry.model&&entry.model.trimEnd)||start;
      return end>start&&now>=start-0.000001&&now<end-0.000001;
    });
    if(!entries.length)return null;
    if(entries.length===1)return {id:entries[0].id,characterIndex:entries[0].characterIndex};
    // v258: stacked clips (x on top, y underneath, same time coverage). If the clip that is
    // already selected still covers the playhead, it stays selected for the whole slide  - 
    // the border never jumps to the other clip. Only when the selected clip no longer covers
    // the playhead does the pointer's lane pick a new one.
    const keepRef=currentSelectedRef();
    if(keepRef){
      const keepOwner=window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionCharacterIndex;
      const keepEntry=entries.find(entry=>String(entry.id)===keepRef&&(entry.characterIndex==null||keepOwner==null||String(entry.characterIndex)===String(keepOwner)));
      if(keepEntry)return {id:keepEntry.id,characterIndex:keepEntry.characterIndex};
    }
    // Multiple clips can occupy separate vertical sub-lanes at the same time. Use the
    // pointer's lane only to choose between those data-matched candidates; time membership
    // always comes from trimStart/trimEnd, never horizontal DOM rectangles.
    const hit=document.elementFromPoint(x,y);
    const hitClip=hit&&hit.closest?hit.closest('.anim-clip,.anim-clip-readonly,.overlay-clip,.track-clip[data-bg-id]'):null;
    const hitTop=hitClip?Number.parseFloat(hitClip.style.top||'0'):NaN;
    let best=entries[0],bestScore=Infinity;
    entries.forEach(entry=>{
      const el=timelineEntryElement(row,entry);
      const top=el?Number.parseFloat(el.style.top||'0'):NaN;
      const score=Number.isFinite(hitTop)&&Number.isFinite(top)?Math.abs(top-hitTop):0;
      if(score<bestScore){best=entry;bestScore=score;}
    });
    return {id:best.id,characterIndex:best.characterIndex};
  }
  window.__ethvelleResolveTimelineClipAtTime=resolveTimelineClipAtTime;
  function autoSelectAtPlayhead(){
    if(typeof isPlaying!=='undefined'&&isPlaying)return;
    // A clip's own move/trim gesture owns selection until release. The only exception
    // is an actual playhead scrub, where the lock must yield to the clip under the playhead.
    const selectionLock=String(window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionLockId||'');
    if(!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing){
      const activeManipulator=document.querySelector('#timelineInner .dragging,#timelineInner [data-ev-timeline-drag-active="1"]');
      if(selectionLock||activeManipulator)return;
    }
    const ref=currentSelectedRef();
    const pointer=window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer;
    // A quick tap on a clip selects that clip but must not immediately be replaced by the
    // clip currently beneath the playhead. Auto-selection is reserved for real scrub drags.
    if(!window.__ethvelleTimelineScrubbing&&pointer&&!pointer.moved)return;
    // Stay in the row where the slide STARTED; a finger drifting over the fox's row must not switch rows.
    const pointerRow=pointer&&pointer.active?((pointer.startRow&&pointer.startRow.isConnected)?pointer.startRow:rowForPointer(pointer.x,pointer.y)):null;
    const selected=selectedElementForRef(ref);
    let row=pointerRow||selected?.closest('.layer-row')||null;
    if(!row&&ref){
      const char=ref.match(/^char(\d+)$/);
      if(char)row=$('timelineInner')?.querySelector('.char-row[data-char-idx="'+char[1]+'"]')||null;
    }
    if(!row)return;
    const x=pointer&&pointer.active?pointer.x:0;
    const y=pointer&&pointer.active?pointer.y:0;
    const result=resolveTimelineClipAtTime(row,x,y);
    if(!result||String(result.id)===String(ref)&&String(result.characterIndex??'')===String(window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionCharacterIndex??''))return;
    if(typeof window.__ethvelleLightSelectTimelineClip==='function')window.__ethvelleLightSelectTimelineClip(result.id,result.characterIndex);
  }
  let selectRaf=0,forceSelectPending=false;
  function scheduleAutoSelect(force=false){
    // Selection is cheap while a gesture is live: resolve clip membership from model time
    // once per display frame and paint only the selection chrome. Heavy toolbar/keyframe/media
    // synchronization is deferred to the single pointer-up commit.
    if(force)forceSelectPending=true;
    if(selectRaf)return;
    selectRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      selectRaf=0;
      autoSelectAtPlayhead();
      if(forceSelectPending){
        forceSelectPending=false;
        if(typeof window.__ethvelleCommitTimelineSelection==='function')window.__ethvelleCommitTimelineSelection();
        if(window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer)window.__ethvelleTimelineSelectionPointer.active=false;
      }
    });
  }
  const timeline=$('timelineScroll');
  if(timeline&&!timeline.dataset.evcaV74AutoSelect){
    timeline.dataset.evcaV74AutoSelect='1';
    let lastLeft=timeline.scrollLeft;
    timeline.addEventListener('scroll',()=>{
      const left=timeline.scrollLeft;
      if(Math.abs(left-lastLeft)>.25){lastLeft=left;scheduleAutoSelect(false);}
    },{passive:true});
    // Commit selection immediately after a gesture; during movement the expensive row scan
    // is throttled because the playhead/canvas remain independently frame-smooth.
    timeline.addEventListener('pointerup',()=>scheduleAutoSelect(true),{passive:true});
    timeline.addEventListener('touchend',()=>scheduleAutoSelect(true),{passive:true});
  }
  // Scrubbing the playhead also changes the clip crossing the fixed centre line.
  const oldSetTime=window.setCurrentAbsoluteTime;
  if(typeof oldSetTime==='function'&&!oldSetTime.__evcaV75AutoSelect){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldSetTime.apply(this,arguments);scheduleAutoSelect();return r;};
    wrapped.__evcaV75AutoSelect=true;window.setCurrentAbsoluteTime=wrapped;
  }

  /* Force sharp character-row corners after each dynamic timeline render. This is style-only:
     the observer watches child replacement, not style attributes, so it cannot feed back. */
  function forceSquareCharacterRows(){
    const rows=$('characterRows');if(!rows)return;
    qa(rows,'.char-row,.char-row .char-row-track,.char-row .char-row-track *').forEach(el=>{
      el.style.setProperty('border-radius','0','important');
    });
  }
  const oldRenderChars=window.renderCharacterLayers;
  if(typeof oldRenderChars==='function'&&!oldRenderChars.__evcaV75Square){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldRenderChars.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(forceSquareCharacterRows);return r;};
    wrapped.__evcaV75Square=true;window.renderCharacterLayers=wrapped;
  }
  const oldRenderClips=window.renderTimelineClip;
  if(typeof oldRenderClips==='function'&&!oldRenderClips.__evcaV75Square){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldRenderClips.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(forceSquareCharacterRows);scheduleAutoSelect();return r;};
    wrapped.__evcaV75Square=true;window.renderTimelineClip=wrapped;
  }
  forceSquareCharacterRows();

  // The older v72 script also installs a DOMContentLoaded callback that may recreate
  // Save PNG + Add Layer after this file has already inserted the single Frame button.
  // Run this normalizer after that older callback so the final live toolbar always has one.
  function normalizeV75UI(){
    replaceFrameToolbarItems();
    syncCanvasZoomEntry();
    ensureSingleZoomFrameButton();
    forceSquareCharacterRows();
    scheduleAutoSelect();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',normalizeV75UI,{once:true});
  else normalizeV75UI();
})();
</script>

<script id="ethvelle-v77-editor-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  /* The zoom-out control already exits zoom through its original handler. Upgrade
     only its placeholder glyph, leaving the control's behavior and layout untouched. */
  function cleanZoomHud(){
    const back=$('evcaZoomOut');
    if(!back)return;
    const restoreIcon='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.9" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M8 3v5H3M3 3l6 6"/><path d="M16 3v5h5M21 3l-6 6"/><path d="M3 16v5h5M3 21l6-6"/><path d="M21 16v5h-5M21 21l-6-6"/></svg>';
    const currentSvg=back.querySelector('svg');
    const iconIsCurrent=!!currentSvg&&currentSvg.getAttribute('viewBox')==='0 0 24 24'&&
      currentSvg.querySelector('path')?.getAttribute('d')==='M8 3v5H3M3 3l6 6'&&currentSvg.querySelectorAll('path').length===4;
    if(!iconIsCurrent)back.innerHTML=restoreIcon;
    back.setAttribute('aria-label','Back to original canvas');
    back.title='Back to original canvas';
  }

  function attachZoomHudObserver(){
    const hud=$('evcaInlineZoomHud');
    if(!hud||typeof MutationObserver!=='function'||hud.dataset.evV77IconObserver==='1')return;
    hud.dataset.evV77IconObserver='1';
    new MutationObserver(cleanZoomHud).observe(hud,{childList:true,subtree:true});
    cleanZoomHud();
  }
  if(typeof MutationObserver==='function'&&document.body){
    new MutationObserver(attachZoomHudObserver).observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  }
  attachZoomHudObserver();
  cleanZoomHud();

  const oldToggle=window.toggleCanvasZoomView;
  if(typeof oldToggle==='function'&&!oldToggle.__evv77ZoomIcon){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const result=oldToggle.apply(this,arguments);
      requestAnimationFrame(cleanZoomHud);
      return result;
    };
    wrapped.__evv77ZoomIcon=true;
    window.toggleCanvasZoomView=wrapped;
  }
})();
</script>

    position:sticky!important;top:0!important;left:0!important;display:block!important;visibility:visible!important;
    opacity:1!important;width:2px!important;min-width:2px!important;max-width:2px!important;

<script id="ethvelle-v80-final-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const getSelectedId=()=>String(typeof selectedClip==='undefined'||selectedClip==null?'':selectedClip);
  const getOverlay=id=>{try{const list=(typeof overlays!=='undefined'&&Array.isArray(overlays))?overlays:(Array.isArray(window.overlays)?window.overlays:[]);return list.find(o=>o&&String(o.id)===String(id))||null;}catch(_e){return null;}};
  const getObjectClip=id=>typeof window.ethvelleObjectClipById==='function'?window.ethvelleObjectClipById(id):null;

  // Recalculate the two swatch systems after a toggle or color change.
  window.ethvelleRefreshTextSwatches=function(){
    const root=$('ethvelleTextStylePanel');if(!root)return;
    const id=getSelectedId(),model=getOverlay(id);if(!model)return;
    const enabled={strokeColor:Number(model.strokeWidth)>0,shadowColor:!!model.shadowEnabled,textBackgroundColor:!!model.textBackgroundEnabled};
    root.querySelectorAll('.evts-swatches[data-swatch-key]').forEach(group=>{
      const key=group.dataset.swatchKey,allow=enabled[key]!==false,value=String(model[key]||'').toLowerCase();
      group.querySelectorAll('.evts-swatch[data-color]').forEach(btn=>btn.classList.toggle('active',allow&&String(btn.dataset.color||'').toLowerCase()===value));
    });
  };
  const oldStyleChanged=window.ethvelleTextStyleChanged;
  if(typeof oldStyleChanged==='function'&&!oldStyleChanged.__evv80Swatches){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldStyleChanged.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>{try{window.ethvelleRefreshTextSwatches();if(window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches)window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches();}catch(_e){}});return r;};
    wrapped.__evv80Swatches=true;window.ethvelleTextStyleChanged=wrapped;
  }
  const oldStylePanel=window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel;
  if(typeof oldStylePanel==='function'&&!oldStylePanel.__evv80Swatches){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldStylePanel.apply(this,arguments);requestAnimationFrame(()=>window.ethvelleRefreshTextSwatches());return r;};
    wrapped.__evv80Swatches=true;window.openEthvelleTextStylePanel=wrapped;
  }

  // Restore the object Key button for all keyframe-capable clips, including text.
  function setDisplayStable(el,value){
    if(!el)return;
    if(el.style.getPropertyValue('display')!==value||el.style.getPropertyPriority('display')!=='important')el.style.setProperty('display',value,'important');
  }
  function stabilizeBottomTools(){
    const screen=$('screen-editor'),clipRow=$('bottomToolsClip'),defaultRow=$('bottomToolsDefault'),keyRow=$('bottomToolsKeyframe');
    if(!screen||!clipRow||!defaultRow||!keyRow)return;
    const id=getSelectedId(),kfSelected=typeof selectedKfIdx!=='undefined'&&selectedKfIdx!==null;
    if(kfSelected){setDisplayStable(defaultRow,'none');setDisplayStable(clipRow,'none');setDisplayStable(keyRow,'flex');return;}
    setDisplayStable(keyRow,'none');
    if(!id){setDisplayStable(clipRow,'none');setDisplayStable(defaultRow,'flex');return;}
    setDisplayStable(defaultRow,'none');setDisplayStable(clipRow,'flex');
    const ov=getOverlay(id),bg=Array.isArray(window.backgrounds)?window.backgrounds.find(b=>b&&String(b.id)===id):null;
    const obj=getObjectClip(id),isChar=id==='main'||/^seg\d+$/.test(id),isText=!!ov&&ov.type==='text';
    const key=$('clipKeyTool');setDisplayStable(key,(isChar||!!obj)?'flex':'none');
    const anim=$('clipOverlayAnimationTool');setDisplayStable(anim,((!!ov&&['image','video','text'].includes(ov.type))||isChar)?'flex':'none');
    const filter=$('clipOverlayFilterTool');setDisplayStable(filter,(!!ov&&['image','video','text'].includes(ov.type))||!!bg?'flex':'none');
    ['clipMirrorTool','clipMaskTool','clipEffectsTool'].forEach(toolId=>setDisplayStable($(toolId),(!!ov&&['image','video','text'].includes(ov.type))||!!bg||isChar?'flex':'none'));
    if(isText){
      // Selecting a text clip must behave like selecting any other object layer: keep the
      // shared Key button and object Clip Animations tool, but do not inject six extra text
      // styling actions into the normal bottom row. Text editing opens only from its own entry.
      setDisplayStable($('clipTextEditTool'),'flex');
      ['clipTextFontTool','clipTextStrokeTool','clipTextShadowTool','clipTextColorTool','clipTextBackgroundTool'].forEach(toolId=>setDisplayStable($(toolId),'none'));
      setDisplayStable(key,'flex');
      setDisplayStable(anim,'flex');
    }
  }
  const oldSync=window.syncBottomToolsMode;
  if(typeof oldSync==='function'&&!oldSync.__evv80Toolbar){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldSync.apply(this,arguments);stabilizeBottomTools();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv80Toolbar=true;window.syncBottomToolsMode=wrapped;
  }
  const oldSelect=window.selectClipForChrome;
  if(typeof oldSelect==='function'&&!oldSelect.__evv80Toolbar){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldSelect.apply(this,arguments);scheduleToolbarStabilize();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv80Toolbar=true;window.selectClipForChrome=wrapped;
  }
  const oldDeselect=window.deselectAllClips;
  if(typeof oldDeselect==='function'&&!oldDeselect.__evv80Toolbar){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldDeselect.apply(this,arguments);scheduleToolbarStabilize();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv80Toolbar=true;window.deselectAllClips=wrapped;
  }
  // V80 is the sole bottom-toolbar visibility owner. Other text/audio helpers may still
  // change individual tool styles, so reconcile those changes through one coalesced RAF.
  // setDisplayStable() above is idempotent: this observer's own writes settle immediately
  // instead of fighting another writer or scheduling a frame-by-frame style ping-pong.
  let toolbarStabilizeRaf=0;
  function scheduleToolbarStabilize(){
    if(toolbarStabilizeRaf)return;
    toolbarStabilizeRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{toolbarStabilizeRaf=0;stabilizeBottomTools();});
  }
  const clipToolbar=$('bottomToolsClip');
  if(clipToolbar&&typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    const toolbarObserver=new MutationObserver(scheduleToolbarStabilize);
    toolbarObserver.observe(clipToolbar,{attributes:true,subtree:true,attributeFilter:['style','class']});
  }

  // Pin the thin blue playhead over the complete visible timeline height.
  function restoreTimelinePlayhead(){
    const scroller=$('timelineScroll'),wrap=scroller&&scroller.querySelector(':scope > .playhead-wrap'),line=$('playhead');
    if(!scroller||!line)return;
    if(wrap){wrap.style.setProperty('visibility','visible','important');wrap.style.setProperty('opacity','1','important');}
    line.style.setProperty('display','block','important');line.style.setProperty('visibility','visible','important');
    line.style.setProperty('opacity','1','important');line.style.setProperty('width','2px','important');
    line.style.setProperty('height',Math.max(1,scroller.clientHeight)+'px','important');
    line.style.setProperty('background','#2F5CE8','important');
  }
  const oldSyncPlayhead=window.syncPlayheadHeight;
  if(typeof oldSyncPlayhead==='function'&&!oldSyncPlayhead.__evv80){
    const wrapped=function(){const r=oldSyncPlayhead.apply(this,arguments);restoreTimelinePlayhead();return r;};
    wrapped.__evv80=true;window.syncPlayheadHeight=wrapped;
  }
  if(typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){
    const scroller=$('timelineScroll');if(scroller){const ro=new ResizeObserver(restoreTimelinePlayhead);ro.observe(scroller);}
  }
  if(typeof MutationObserver==='function'){
    const inner=$('timelineInner');if(inner){const mo=new MutationObserver(()=>requestAnimationFrame(restoreTimelinePlayhead));mo.observe(inner,{childList:true,subtree:true});}
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize',restoreTimelinePlayhead,{passive:true});

  // During pointer-drag of a text object, keep the black lower-left resize handle on the
  // selection frame visible and re-anchor it after the editor's own per-frame box update.
  let textPointer=null,dragRaf=0,textPointerClipId=null;
  function scheduleTextHandleRepair(){
    /* v231: the active drag painter already updates its own selection box. */
    if(window.__evTextDragActive)return;
    if(dragRaf)return;
    dragRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      dragRaf=0;if(textPointer===null)return;
      // During a timeline scrub, the playhead can briefly pass outside the selected text
      // clip's active time. The editor then hides its selection overlay for that frame;
      // hold the last valid frame's handles in place until the pointer is released.
      const id=textPointerClipId||getSelectedId(),ov=getOverlay(id);if(!ov||ov.type!=='text')return;
      const layer=$('selBoxLayer');if(!layer)return;
      layer.classList.add('evv80-text-dragging');
      layer.style.setProperty('display','block','important');
      const scale=$('sbScale'),move=$('sbTextMove');
      if(scale)scale.style.setProperty('display','flex','important');
      if(typeof window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners==='function')window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners();
      else if(typeof window.updateSelBox==='function')window.updateSelBox();
    });
  }
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    // Only canvas text-object gestures need the selection-handle repair. A touch that starts
    // on a timeline text clip is a scrub/clip gesture; running updateSelBox + corner repair
    // during every move there caused the multi-layer text-row lag on Android.
    const stageTextTarget=e.target&&e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#overlayMediaLayer .ethvelle-overlay-text-content,#sbTextMove,#sbScale');
    if(!stageTextTarget)return;
    const textId=getSelectedId();
    const model=getOverlay(textId);
    if(model&&model.type==='text'){
      textPointer=e.pointerId;textPointerClipId=textId;scheduleTextHandleRepair();
    }
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{if(textPointer===e.pointerId)scheduleTextHandleRepair();},true);
  function endTextPointer(e){if(textPointer!==e.pointerId)return;textPointer=null;textPointerClipId=null;const layer=$('selBoxLayer');if(layer)layer.classList.remove('evv80-text-dragging');requestAnimationFrame(()=>{if(typeof window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners==='function')window.__ethvelleRepairLayoutCorners();});}
  document.addEventListener('pointerup',endTextPointer,true);
  document.addEventListener('pointercancel',endTextPointer,true);

  // Initial and subsequent syncs. renderFrame keeps the HUD time/play state current while
  // the same compact cluster remains visible in the normal preview and in zoom.
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{stabilizeBottomTools();restoreTimelinePlayhead();window.ethvelleRefreshTextSwatches();if(window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches)window.ethvelleRefreshDockSwatches();});
})();
</script>

<!-- Ethvelle v81: zoom HUD confined to zoomed canvas only, rotation auto-fullscreen,
     and tidied selection-rail chrome (no outer shadow lines, slightly thicker center tick). -->
<style id="ethvelle-v81-final-fixes-css">
  /* The play/pause/scrub HUD belongs to the zoomed canvas only. Without this guard the HUD
     element (a sibling appended once into .canvas-wrap and then left in the DOM) stayed
     visible and interactive on top of the NORMAL canvas after exiting zoom, duplicating the
     transport-row's own play/pause/slider. Hide it whenever .canvas-wrap isn't zoomed. */
  #screen-editor .canvas-wrap:not(.evca-inline-zoom) #evcaInlineZoomHud{
    display:none!important;visibility:hidden!important;opacity:0!important;pointer-events:none!important;
  }
  /* Prevent browser scroll anchoring from shifting the editor when a first clip/row is
     selected or its live markup is rebuilt. Scroll position is managed by the editor. */
  html body #screen-editor #timelineScroll,
  html body #screen-editor #timelineInner,
  html body #screen-editor #characterRows,
  html body #screen-editor #overlayRows,
  html body #screen-editor .layer-row,
  html body #screen-editor .layer-track{overflow-anchor:none!important;}
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v81-final-fixes-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);

  // Rotating the phone to landscape while working on the canvas should behave like a video
  // player going fullscreen: automatically enter the existing inline-zoom canvas view (which
  // already fills the viewport edge-to-edge and hides the timeline/transport/bottom tools),
  // and automatically leave it again on rotating back to portrait. Previously rotation only
  // re-fit the SAME small canvas within the still-visible normal editor chrome, leaving large
  // black bars instead of filling the rotated screen.
  let autoRotateZoomArmed=false; // true only once the device is confirmed in landscape
  function editorIsActive(){
    const screen=$('screen-editor');
    return !!(screen&&screen.classList.contains('active'));
  }
  function deviceIsLandscape(){
    if(window.matchMedia){
      try{return window.matchMedia('(orientation: landscape)').matches;}catch(_e){}
    }
    return window.innerWidth>window.innerHeight;
  }
  function handleAutoRotateZoom(){
    if(!editorIsActive())return;
    // Orientation is a viewport change, not a command to enter or exit zoom.
    // Keep the user's explicit zoom state exactly as-is. The dedicated viewport-fit
    // observer below recomputes the 16:9 stage size while this class remains attached.
    // Preserve the user's explicit zoom state. The v82 viewport observer below already
    // refits the canvas on resize/orientationchange; do not trigger a second canvas rebuild.
    autoRotateZoomArmed=!!document.querySelector('#screen-editor .canvas-wrap.evca-inline-zoom');
  }
  let autoRotateRaf=0;
  function scheduleAutoRotateZoom(){
    if(autoRotateRaf)return;
    autoRotateRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{autoRotateRaf=0;handleAutoRotateZoom();});
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize',scheduleAutoRotateZoom,{passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange',scheduleAutoRotateZoom,{passive:true});
  if(window.visualViewport)window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',scheduleAutoRotateZoom,{passive:true});
  // Also check once on load in case the editor is opened while already in landscape.
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>setTimeout(scheduleAutoRotateZoom,50),{once:true});
  else setTimeout(scheduleAutoRotateZoom,50);
})();
</script>



<!-- Ethvelle v82: background-row selection cleanup, panel-over-playhead stacking,
     and orientation-safe aspect-ratio fitting. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v82-layout-and-selection-css">
  /* Background keeps the same connected white selection frame as every other clip row.
     The previous v82 override hid all four real edge bars, which removed the border itself.
     Any duplicate outline/shadow is suppressed; the four shared .sel-edge elements remain
     the only selection frame, exactly as on Character, Media, Text and Audio clips. */
  html body #screen-editor #timelineInner #track-background .track-clip.selected {
    outline:0!important;
    box-shadow:none!important;
    border-color:transparent!important;
  }
  

  /* The center playhead has z-index:100. Keep the panel and its click-catcher above it,
     so the blue line is fully occluded by every open bottom tool panel. */
  #screen-editor #panelBackdrop.tool-panel-backdrop {
    z-index:100100!important;
  }
  #screen-editor #toolPanel.tool-panel {
    z-index:100101!important;
  }

  /* A canvas frame is a fixed-ratio flex item in both normal and zoomed views. */
  #screen-editor .canvas-wrap #canvasStage {
    flex:0 0 auto!important;
    min-width:0!important;
    min-height:0!important;
  }
</style>
<script id="ethvelle-v82-viewport-fit-js">
(function(){
  'use strict';
  const screen=document.getElementById('screen-editor');
  const wrap=screen&&screen.querySelector('.canvas-wrap');
  const stage=document.getElementById('canvasStage');
  if(!screen||!wrap||!stage)return;

  let fitRaf=0;
  function activeRatio(){
    try{
      const value=(typeof currentProject!=='undefined'&&currentProject&&currentProject.ratio)||'16:9';
      const match=String(value).match(/^\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*:\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*$/);
      return match&&Number(match[1])>0&&Number(match[2])>0 ? `${Number(match[1])}:${Number(match[2])}` : '16:9';
    }catch(_e){return '16:9';}
  }
  function refit(){
    fitRaf=0;
    if(!screen.classList.contains('active'))return;
    const ratio=activeRatio();
    const parts=ratio.split(':').map(Number);
    const rw=parts[0],rh=parts[1];
    if(!(rw>0&&rh>0))return;

    // Set the non-shrinking flex behaviour before measuring/re-sizing. setupCanvasStage
    // computes the largest frame that fits both available dimensions while preserving ratio.
    stage.style.flex='0 0 auto';
    stage.style.aspectRatio=rw+' / '+rh;
    if(typeof setupCanvasStage==='function')setupCanvasStage(ratio);

    // Wait for the WebView to commit the final post-rotation layout before repainting.
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      if(!screen.classList.contains('active'))return;
      // renderFrame already invokes drawStick, so avoid drawing the stage twice on rotation.
      try{if(typeof renderFrame==='function')renderFrame();}catch(_e){}
      try{if(typeof updateSelBox==='function')updateSelBox();}catch(_e){}
    });
  }
  function scheduleRefit(){
    if(fitRaf)return;
    // Two frames let Android/WebView finish applying the new visual viewport dimensions.
    fitRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
      fitRaf=requestAnimationFrame(refit);
    });
  }

  // The observer catches the actual editor preview-area resize, including address-bar and
  // orientation changes that do not always arrive as a reliable window.resize event.
  if(typeof ResizeObserver==='function'){
    const observer=new ResizeObserver(scheduleRefit);
    observer.observe(wrap);
  }
  window.addEventListener('resize',scheduleRefit,{passive:true});
  window.addEventListener('orientationchange',scheduleRefit,{passive:true});
  if(window.visualViewport)window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',scheduleRefit,{passive:true});

  // Re-fit after the editor becomes visible; the initial observer notification can occur
  // while its parent screen is display:none.
  const editorObserver=new MutationObserver(()=>{
    if(screen.classList.contains('active'))scheduleRefit();
  });
  editorObserver.observe(screen,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  scheduleRefit();
})();
</script>

    display:block!important;visibility:visible!important;opacity:1!important;position:absolute!important;

  }

  html body #screen-editor #timelineInner #track-background .track-clip.selected > .anim-clip-handle::before,

<!-- Ethvelle v84: lower-cost zoom preview, centered transport, matching scrub rail,
     transform/keyframe editing repair, and immediate timeline gesture recovery. -->
<style id="ethvelle-v84-zoom-transport-css">
